Surprisingly, these policies succeed by generating adversarial outputs that deceive other LLM-judges and score well on benchmarks like Arena-Hard, revealing both potential and risks in deploying reasoning judges.
为将推理LLM作为裁判应用于无法直接验证输出正确性的领域，本研究系统评估了推理LLM裁判在强化学习对齐中的实际效果。在由gpt-oss-120b黄金标准裁判训练小规模裁判的控制合成环境中，研究对比了非推理与推理裁判的表现。结果表明，非推理裁判易导致奖励欺骗，而推理裁判能生成在黄金标准下表现优异的策略。有趣的是，这些策略通过生成能欺骗其他LLM裁判且在Arena-Hard等基准上得分高的对抗性输出实现高表现，揭示了推理裁判应用中的潜力与风险。
One Model, Many Budgets: Elastic Latent Interfaces for Diffusion Transformers
Authors: Moayed Haji-Ali, Willi Menapace, Ivan Skorokhodov, Dogyun Park, Anil Kag, Michael Vasilkovsky, Sergey Tulyakov, Vicente Ordonez, Aliaksandr Siarohin
First: 2026-03-12T17:57:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:57:04+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://snap-research.github.io/elit/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Diffusion transformers (DiTs) achieve high generative quality but lock FLOPs to image resolution, limiting principled latency-quality trade-offs, and allocate computation uniformly across input spatial tokens, wasting resource allocation to unimportant regions. We introduce Elastic Latent Interface Transformer (ELIT), a drop-in, DiT-compatible mechanism that decouples input image size from compute. Our approach inserts a latent interface, a learnable variable-length token sequence on which standard transformer blocks can operate. Lightweight Read and Write cross-attention layers move information between spatial tokens and latents and prioritize important input regions. By training with random dropping of tail latents, ELIT learns to produce importance-ordered representations with earlier latents capturing global structure while later ones contain information to refine details. At inference, the number of latents can be dynamically adjusted to match compute constraints. ELIT is deliberately minimal, adding two cross-attention layers while leaving the rectified flow objective and the DiT stack unchanged. Across datasets and architectures (DiT, U-ViT, HDiT, MM-DiT), ELIT delivers consistent gains. On ImageNet-1K 512px, ELIT delivers an average gain of $35.3\%$ and $39.6\%$ in FID and FDD scores. Project page: https://snap-research.github.io/elit/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the rigid compute-resolution coupling in diffusion transformers that hinders efficient latency-quality trade-offs, the authors propose ELIT, a minimal drop-in mechanism that decouples image size from computational cost by introducing a learnable latent interface. This interface, a variable-length token sequence, enables transformer blocks to operate on compressed representations, with lightweight read/write cross-attention layers transferring information between spatial tokens and latents while prioritizing important regions. Trained via random dropping of tail latents, ELIT learns to encode global structure early and fine-grained details later, allowing dynamic adjustment of latent count at inference to match compute budgets. Across multiple DiT variants and datasets, ELIT consistently improves quality: on ImageNet-1K 512px, it reduces FID by 35.3% and FDD by 39.6% without altering the core DiT architecture.
为了解决扩散Transformer中计算量与图像分辨率强绑定、难以实现延迟与质量权衡的问题，作者提出ELIT机制，通过引入可学习的可变长度潜在接口，将图像尺寸与计算量解耦。该接口作为令牌序列，使标准Transformer块可操作压缩表示，轻量级读写交叉注意力层在空间令牌与潜在表示间传递信息并优先处理重要区域。通过随机丢弃尾部潜在令牌训练，ELIT学会早期编码全局结构、后期编码细节，推理时可根据计算预算动态调整潜在令牌数量。在多个DiT变体和数据集上，ELIT持续提升生成质量：在ImageNet-1K 512px上，FID降低35.3%，FDD降低39.6%，且未改变DiT核心架构。
Separable neural architectures as a primitive for unified predictive and generative intelligence
Authors: Reza T. Batley, Apurba Sarker, Rajib Mostakim, Andrew Klichine, Sourav Saha
First: 2026-03-12T17:56:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:56:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Intelligent systems across physics, language and perception often exhibit factorisable structure, yet are typically modelled by monolithic neural architectures that do not explicitly exploit this structure. The separable neural architecture (SNA) addresses this by formalising a representational class that unifies additive, quadratic and tensor-decomposed neural models. By constraining interaction order and tensor rank, SNAs impose a structural inductive bias that factorises high-dimensional mappings into low-arity components. Separability need not be a property of the system itself: it often emerges in the coordinates or representations through which the system is expressed. Crucially, this coordinate-aware formulation reveals a structural analogy between chaotic spatiotemporal dynamics and linguistic autoregression. By treating continuous physical states as smooth, separable embeddings, SNAs enable distributional modelling of chaotic systems. This approach mitigates the nonphysical drift characteristics of deterministic operators whilst remaining applicable to discrete sequences. The compositional versatility of this approach is demonstrated across four domains: autonomous waypoint navigation via reinforcement learning, inverse generation of multifunctional microstructures, distributional modelling of turbulent flow and neural language modelling. These results establish the separable neural architecture as a domain-agnostic primitive for predictive and generative intelligence, capable of unifying both deterministic and distributional representations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可分离神经架构作为统一预测与生成智能的基本构件
在物理、语言和感知等领域，智能系统常表现出可分解的结构，但通常被建模为不显式利用该结构的单体神经架构。可分离神经架构（SNA）通过形式化一个统一加性、二次型和张量分解神经模型的表示类来解决这一问题。通过约束交互阶数和张量秩，SNA施加了一种结构性归纳偏置，将高维映射分解为低元组分量。可分离性不必是系统本身的属性：它常在系统所表达的坐标或表示中显现。关键的是，这种坐标感知的表述揭示了混沌时空动力学与语言自回归之间的一种结构类比。通过将连续物理状态视为平滑、可分离的嵌入，SNA能够对混沌系统进行分布建模。该方法缓解了确定性算子的非物理漂移特性，同时仍适用于离散序列。该方法的组合灵活性在四个领域得到验证：基于强化学习的自主航点导航、多功能微结构的逆向生成、湍流流动的分布建模和神经语言建模。这些结果确立了可分离神经架构作为预测与生成智能的领域无关基本构件，能够统一确定性和分布式表示。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that intelligent systems across domains often exhibit factorisable structure but are typically modeled by monolithic neural networks, the paper introduces separable neural architectures (SNAs) as a unifying primitive. SNAs formalize a class of models that decompose high-dimensional mappings into low-arity components via structural inductive bias, enabling efficient representation of additive, quadratic, and tensor-decomposed models. The approach leverages coordinate-aware formulations to reveal analogies between chaotic dynamics and linguistic autoregression, allowing SNAs to model both continuous physical systems and discrete sequences without nonphysical drift. Experimental results across four domains—autonomous navigation, microstructure generation, turbulent flow modeling, and language modeling—demonstrate the versatility and effectiveness of SNAs in unifying predictive and generative tasks across deterministic and distributional settings.
鉴于跨领域智能系统常具可分解结构，但通常被单一大型神经网络建模，本文提出可分离神经架构（SNA）作为统一的原始构件。SNA通过结构化归纳偏置，将高维映射分解为低阶分量，支持加性、二次和张量分解模型的统一表达。该方法利用坐标感知形式揭示混沌时空动态与语言自回归间的结构类比，使SNA能有效建模连续物理系统与离散序列，避免非物理漂移。在四个领域——自主导航、多功能微结构逆生成、湍流建模与语言建模——的实验结果表明，SNA具备统一预测与生成能力，适用于确定性与分布式表示，展现出跨域通用性。
HandelBot: Real-World Piano Playing via Fast Adaptation of Dexterous Robot Policies
Authors: Amber Xie, Haozhi Qi, Dorsa Sadigh
First: 2026-03-12T17:56:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:56:29+00:00
Comments: Website: https://amberxie88.github.io/handelbot
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Mastering dexterous manipulation with multi-fingered hands has been a grand challenge in robotics for decades. Despite its potential, the difficulty of collecting high-quality data remains a primary bottleneck for high-precision tasks. While reinforcement learning and simulation-to-real-world transfer offer a promising alternative, the transferred policies often fail for tasks demanding millimeter-scale precision, such as bimanual piano playing. In this work, we introduce HandelBot, a framework that combines a simulation policy and rapid adaptation through a two-stage pipeline. Starting from a simulation-trained policy, we first apply a structured refinement stage to correct spatial alignments by adjusting lateral finger joints based on physical rollouts. Next, we use residual reinforcement learning to autonomously learn fine-grained corrective actions. Through extensive hardware experiments across five recognized songs, we demonstrate that HandelBot can successfully perform precise bimanual piano playing. Our system outperforms direct simulation deployment by a factor of 1.8x and requires only 30 minutes of physical interaction data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HandelBot：通过灵巧机器人策略的快速适应实现真实世界钢琴演奏
多指灵巧操作的掌握一直是机器人领域数十年来的重要挑战。尽管其潜力巨大，但高质量数据的收集难度仍是高精度任务的主要瓶颈。虽然强化学习和仿真到现实的迁移提供了有前景的替代方案，但迁移后的策略在要求毫米级精度的任务（如双手弹钢琴）中往往失败。在本研究中，我们提出了HandelBot框架，该框架结合了仿真策略与通过两阶段管道实现的快速适应。从仿真训练策略出发，我们首先应用结构化优化阶段，通过调整侧向手指关节以纠正空间对齐，基于物理滚动进行调整；随后，我们使用残差强化学习自主学习精细的修正动作。通过在五首公认歌曲上进行大量硬件实验，我们证明HandelBot能够成功实现精确的双手钢琴演奏。我们的系统性能比直接部署仿真策略高出1.8倍，且仅需30分钟的物理交互数据。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of achieving millimeter-scale precision in dexterous robotic manipulation, particularly for bimanual piano playing, this work introduces HandelBot, a two-stage framework that adapts simulation-trained policies to real-world hardware. The method first refines spatial alignment via structured adjustments to lateral finger joints using physical rollouts, then employs residual reinforcement learning to autonomously learn fine-grained corrective actions. Experimental results across five songs show HandelBot successfully executes precise piano playing, outperforming direct simulation deployment by 1.8x and requiring only 30 minutes of physical interaction data.
为实现毫米级精度的灵巧机器人操作，特别是双臂钢琴演奏，本文提出HandelBot框架，通过两阶段方法将仿真训练策略快速适配至真实硬件。方法首先通过物理滚动调整侧向指关节以校正空间对齐，再利用残差强化学习自主学习精细修正动作。在五首曲目实验中，HandelBot成功完成精准钢琴演奏，性能比直接仿真部署提升1.8倍，仅需30分钟真实交互数据。
BiGain: Unified Token Compression for Joint Generation and Classification
Authors: Jiacheng Liu, Shengkun Tang, Jiacheng Cui, Dongkuan Xu, Zhiqiang Shen
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-12T17:55:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:55:53+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026. Code: https://github.com/Greenoso/BiGain
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Acceleration methods for diffusion models (e.g., token merging or downsampling) typically optimize synthesis quality under reduced compute, yet often ignore discriminative capacity. We revisit token compression with a joint objective and present BiGain, a training-free, plug-and-play framework that preserves generation quality while improving classification in accelerated diffusion models. Our key insight is frequency separation: mapping feature-space signals into a frequency-aware representation disentangles fine detail from global semantics, enabling compression that respects both generative fidelity and discriminative utility. BiGain reflects this principle with two frequency-aware operators: (1) Laplacian-gated token merging, which encourages merges among spectrally smooth tokens while discouraging merges of high-contrast tokens, thereby retaining edges and textures; and (2) Interpolate-Extrapolate KV Downsampling, which downsamples keys/values via a controllable interextrapolation between nearest and average pooling while keeping queries intact, thereby conserving attention precision. Across DiT- and U-Net-based backbones and ImageNet-1K, ImageNet-100, Oxford-IIIT Pets, and COCO-2017, our operators consistently improve the speed-accuracy trade-off for diffusion-based classification, while maintaining or enhancing generation quality under comparable acceleration. For instance, on ImageNet-1K, with 70% token merging on Stable Diffusion 2.0, BiGain increases classification accuracy by 7.15% while improving FID by 0.34 (1.85%). Our analyses indicate that balanced spectral retention, preserving high-frequency detail and low/mid-frequency semantics, is a reliable design rule for token compression in diffusion models. To our knowledge, BiGain is the first framework to jointly study and advance both generation and classification under accelerated diffusion, supporting lower-cost deployment.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to balance generation quality and classification performance in accelerated diffusion models, BiGain introduces a training-free, plug-and-play framework that jointly optimizes both tasks. It achieves this through frequency-aware token compression: Laplacian-gated token merging preserves high-contrast features by discouraging merges of spectrally distinct tokens, while Interpolate-Extrapolate KV Downsampling maintains attention precision by selectively interpolating or extrapolating key-value downsampling while keeping queries unchanged. Experimental results across multiple backbones and datasets show consistent improvements in the speed-accuracy trade-off, with notable gains on ImageNet-1K where 70% token merging led to a 7.15% accuracy boost and a 0.34 FID improvement (1.85%), while preserving or enhancing generation quality.
为在加速扩散模型中同时优化生成质量与分类性能，BiGain 提出了一种无需训练、即插即用的框架，通过频域感知的令牌压缩实现联合优化。其核心方法包括拉普拉斯门控令牌合并，通过鼓励频谱平滑令牌合并、抑制高对比度令牌合并，保留边缘与纹理；以及插值-外推式 KV 下采样，通过在最近邻与平均池化间可控插值/外推下采样键值对，保持查询不变，从而保留注意力精度。实验表明，该框架在多个骨干网络和数据集上均显著提升速度-精度权衡，例如在 ImageNet-1K 上，70% 令牌合并下分类准确率提升 7.15%，FID 提升 0.34（1.85%），同时保持或增强生成质量。
STAMP: Selective Task-Aware Mechanism for Text Privacy
Authors: Fengwei Tian, Payel Bhattacharjee, Heidi Hanson, Geoffrey D. Rubin, Joseph Y. Lo, Ravi Tandon
First: 2026-03-12T17:55:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:55:07+00:00
Comments: EACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present STAMP (Selective Task-Aware Mechanism for Text Privacy), a new framework for task-aware text privatization that achieves an improved privacy-utility trade-off. STAMP selectively allocates privacy budgets across tokens by jointly considering (i) each token's importance to the downstream task (as measured via a task- or query-specific representation), and (ii) its privacy sensitivity (e.g., names, dates, identifiers). This token-level partitioning enables fine-grained, group-wise control over the level of noise applied to different parts of the input, balancing privacy protection with task relevance. To privatize individual token embeddings, we introduce the polar mechanism, which perturbs only the direction of embeddings on the unit sphere while preserving their magnitude. Decoding is performed via cosine nearest-neighbor search, aligning the perturbation geometry with the decoding geometry. Unlike isotropic noise mechanisms, the polar mechanism maintains semantic neighborhoods in the embedding space and better preserves downstream utility. Experimental evaluations on SQuAD, Yelp, and AG News datasets demonstrate that STAMP, when combined with the normalized polar mechanism, consistently achieves superior privacy-utility trade-offs across varying per-token privacy budgets.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to balance privacy and utility in text processing, STAMP introduces a task-aware framework that allocates privacy budgets selectively per token based on both task relevance and privacy sensitivity. It employs a polar mechanism to perturb token embeddings by altering their direction on the unit sphere while preserving magnitude, enabling semantic neighborhood preservation during decoding via cosine nearest-neighbor search. Experiments on SQuAD, Yelp, and AG News show that STAMP, especially with the normalized polar mechanism, consistently outperforms baseline methods in privacy-utility trade-offs across varying token-level privacy budgets.
为在文本处理中平衡隐私与效用，STAMP提出一种任务感知框架，按每个词元的任务相关性与隐私敏感性动态分配隐私预算。其采用极坐标机制，仅扰动词元嵌入的方向（保持模长不变），并结合余弦最近邻搜索进行解码，以维持语义邻域结构。在SQuAD、Yelp和AG News数据集上的实验表明，STAMP（尤其配合归一化极坐标机制）在不同词元级隐私预算下，始终优于基线方法，实现更优的隐私-效用权衡。
SceneAssistant: A Visual Feedback Agent for Open-Vocabulary 3D Scene Generation
Authors: Jun Luo, Jiaxiang Tang, Ruijie Lu, Gang Zeng
First: 2026-03-12T17:55:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:55:07+00:00
Comments: Code: https://github.com/ROUJINN/SceneAssistant
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Text-to-3D scene generation from natural language is highly desirable for digital content creation. However, existing methods are largely domain-restricted or reliant on predefined spatial relationships, limiting their capacity for unconstrained, open-vocabulary 3D scene synthesis. In this paper, we introduce SceneAssistant, a visual-feedback-driven agent designed for open-vocabulary 3D scene generation. Our framework leverages modern 3D object generation model along with the spatial reasoning and planning capabilities of Vision-Language Models (VLMs). To enable open-vocabulary scene composition, we provide the VLMs with a comprehensive set of atomic operations (e.g., Scale, Rotate, FocusOn). At each interaction step, the VLM receives rendered visual feedback and takes actions accordingly, iteratively refining the scene to achieve more coherent spatial arrangements and better alignment with the input text. Experimental results demonstrate that our method can generate diverse, open-vocabulary, and high-quality 3D scenes. Both qualitative analysis and quantitative human evaluations demonstrate the superiority of our approach over existing methods. Furthermore, our method allows users to instruct the agent to edit existing scenes based on natural language commands. Our code is available at https://github.com/ROUJINN/SceneAssistant
中文标题/摘要
标题：SceneAssistant：面向开放词汇3D场景生成的视觉反馈代理
从自然语言生成3D场景对数字内容创作具有高度需求。然而，现有方法大多受限于特定领域或依赖预定义的空间关系，限制了其在无约束、开放词汇3D场景合成中的能力。本文提出SceneAssistant，一种面向开放词汇3D场景生成的视觉反馈驱动代理。我们的框架结合现代3D物体生成模型与视觉-语言模型（VLMs）的空间推理和规划能力。为实现开放词汇场景组合，我们为VLMs提供一套完整的原子操作（如缩放、旋转、聚焦）。在每次交互步骤中，VLM接收渲染后的视觉反馈并据此采取行动，迭代优化场景，以实现更连贯的空间布局和更贴近输入文本的语义对齐。实验结果表明，我们的方法可生成多样、开放词汇且高质量的3D场景。定性分析与定量人工评估均证明我们的方法优于现有方法。此外，我们的方法允许用户通过自然语言指令指导代理编辑现有场景。代码开源于https://github.com/ROUJINN/SceneAssistant
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for flexible, open-vocabulary 3D scene generation from natural language, this paper introduces SceneAssistant, a visual-feedback-driven agent that combines 3D object generation models with Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for iterative scene composition. The VLM is equipped with atomic operations like Scale, Rotate, and FocusOn to enable open-vocabulary synthesis, and at each step, it receives rendered visual feedback to refine spatial arrangements. Experiments show that SceneAssistant produces diverse, high-quality 3D scenes that better align with input text compared to existing methods, and it also supports natural-language editing of existing scenes.
为实现从自然语言到开放词汇3D场景的灵活生成，本文提出SceneAssistant，一种基于视觉反馈的代理系统，结合3D物体生成模型与视觉-语言模型（VLM），通过原子操作（如缩放、旋转、聚焦）迭代构建场景。VLM在每一步接收渲染图像反馈，动态调整空间布局以更贴合输入文本。实验表明，该方法生成的场景多样、高质量，优于现有方法，且支持用户通过自然语言指令编辑现有场景。
Incremental Neural Network Verification via Learned Conflicts
Authors: Raya Elsaleh, Liam Davis, Haoze Wu, Guy Katz
First: 2026-03-12T17:52:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:52:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural network verification is often used as a core component within larger analysis procedures, which generate sequences of closely related verification queries over the same network. In existing neural network verifiers, each query is typically solved independently, and information learned during previous runs is discarded, leading to repeated exploration of the same infeasible regions of the search space. In this work, we aim to expedite verification by reducing this redundancy. We propose an incremental verification technique that reuses learned conflicts across related verification queries. The technique can be added on top of any branch-and-bound-based neural network verifier. During verification, the verifier records conflicts corresponding to learned infeasible combinations of activation phases, and retains them across runs. We formalize a refinement relation between verification queries and show that conflicts learned for a query remain valid under refinement, enabling sound conflict inheritance. Inherited conflicts are handled using a SAT solver to perform consistency checks and propagation, allowing infeasible subproblems to be detected and pruned early during search. We implement the proposed technique in the Marabou verifier and evaluate it on three verification tasks: local robustness radius determination, verification with input splitting, and minimal sufficient feature set extraction. Our experiments show that incremental conflict reuse reduces verification effort and yields speedups of up to $1.9\times$ over a non-incremental baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过学习冲突实现神经网络的增量验证
神经网络验证常作为大型分析流程的核心组件，生成针对同一网络的一系列密切相关验证查询。现有验证器通常独立求解每个查询，且丢弃先前运行中获得的信息，导致重复探索搜索空间中的相同不可行区域。本工作旨在通过减少这种冗余来加速验证。我们提出一种增量验证技术，可在相关验证查询间重用已学习的冲突。该技术可叠加在任何基于分支定界法的神经网络验证器之上。验证过程中，验证器记录与已学习不可行激活相组合相关的冲突，并在多次运行间保留这些冲突。我们形式化了验证查询间的精炼关系，并证明对某一查询学习到的冲突在精炼后仍保持有效，从而实现安全的冲突继承。继承的冲突通过SAT求解器进行一致性检查和传播，允许在搜索过程中早期检测并剪枝不可行子问题。我们在Marabou验证器中实现该技术，并在三个验证任务上进行评估：局部鲁棒半径确定、输入分割验证及最小充分特征集提取。实验表明，增量冲突重用减少了验证开销，相比非增量基线方法最高可提速1.9倍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of solving neural network verification queries independently and discarding learned information, this work proposes an incremental verification method that reuses conflicts across related queries. The technique integrates with any branch-and-bound verifier by recording and preserving infeasible activation phase combinations as conflicts, which remain valid under query refinement. These conflicts are then leveraged via a SAT solver to prune infeasible subproblems early, reducing redundant search. Evaluated on three tasks including robustness radius estimation and feature set extraction, the method achieves up to 1.9x speedup over a non-incremental baseline.
为减少神经网络验证中重复探索无效区域的开销，该研究提出一种增量验证方法，可在相关查询间复用已学习的冲突信息。该方法兼容任何基于分支定界框架的验证器，通过记录并保留激活阶段组合的不可行冲突，在查询细化时保持其有效性，并借助SAT求解器进行一致性检查与传播，提前剪枝无效子问题。在局部鲁棒半径、输入分割验证及最小特征集提取三个任务上，该方法相较非增量基线最高提速1.9倍。
Temporal Straightening for Latent Planning
Authors: Ying Wang, Oumayma Bounou, Gaoyue Zhou, Randall Balestriero, Tim G. J. Rudner, Yann LeCun, Mengye Ren
First: 2026-03-12T17:49:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:49:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning good representations is essential for latent planning with world models. While pretrained visual encoders produce strong semantic visual features, they are not tailored to planning and contain information irrelevant -- or even detrimental -- to planning. Inspired by the perceptual straightening hypothesis in human visual processing, we introduce temporal straightening to improve representation learning for latent planning. Using a curvature regularizer that encourages locally straightened latent trajectories, we jointly learn an encoder and a predictor. We show that reducing curvature this way makes the Euclidean distance in latent space a better proxy for the geodesic distance and improves the conditioning of the planning objective. We demonstrate empirically that temporal straightening makes gradient-based planning more stable and yields significantly higher success rates across a suite of goal-reaching tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于潜在规划的时间直化
学习良好的表征对于世界模型的潜在规划至关重要。虽然预训练的视觉编码器能生成强语义视觉特征，但它们并未针对规划进行优化，且包含与规划无关甚至有害的信息。受人类视觉处理中感知直化假说的启发，我们引入时间直化以改进潜在规划的表征学习。通过一个曲率正则化项，鼓励局部直化的潜在轨迹，我们联合学习编码器和预测器。我们表明，通过这种方式减少曲率，能使潜在空间中的欧氏距离更好地近似测地线距离，并改善规划目标的条件数。实验证明，时间直化使基于梯度的规划更加稳定，并在一系列目标到达任务中显著提高成功率。
Summary / 总结
To enhance latent planning, the paper addresses the limitation of pretrained visual encoders that, while semantically strong, are not optimized for planning and may contain irrelevant or harmful information. Inspired by perceptual straightening in human vision, the authors propose temporal straightening: a method that jointly learns an encoder and predictor using a curvature regularizer to encourage locally straightened latent trajectories. This approach improves the Euclidean distance in latent space as a proxy for geodesic distance and enhances planning stability. Experiments show that temporal straightening significantly boosts success rates in gradient-based planning across multiple goal-reaching tasks.
为提升潜在规划效果，该论文指出预训练视觉编码器虽具备强语义特征，但未针对规划优化，可能包含干扰规划的信息。受人类视觉感知直化假说启发，作者提出时间直化方法：通过曲率正则化联合学习编码器与预测器，使潜在空间轨迹局部趋于直线化。该方法使潜在空间中的欧氏距离更准确反映测地距离，提升规划目标的稳定性。实验表明，时间直化显著提高梯度规划在多个目标达成任务中的成功率。
Security Considerations for Artificial Intelligence Agents
Authors: Ninghui Li, Kaiyuan Zhang, Kyle Polley, Jerry Ma
First: 2026-03-12T17:49:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:49:39+00:00
Comments: Perplexity Response to NIST/CAISI Request for Information 2025-0035. 91 Fed. Reg. 698 (Jan. 8, 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This article, a lightly adapted version of Perplexity's response to NIST/CAISI Request for Information 2025-0035, details our observations and recommendations concerning the security of frontier AI agents. These insights are informed by Perplexity's experience operating general-purpose agentic systems used by millions of users and thousands of enterprises in both controlled and open-world environments. Agent architectures change core assumptions around code-data separation, authority boundaries, and execution predictability, creating new confidentiality, integrity, and availability failure modes. We map principal attack surfaces across tools, connectors, hosting boundaries, and multi-agent coordination, with particular emphasis on indirect prompt injection, confused-deputy behavior, and cascading failures in long-running workflows. We then assess current defenses as a layered stack: input-level and model-level mitigations, sandboxed execution, and deterministic policy enforcement for high-consequence actions. Finally, we identify standards and research gaps, including adaptive security benchmarks, policy models for delegation and privilege control, and guidance for secure multi-agent system design aligned with NIST risk management principles.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能代理的安全考量
本文是Perplexity对NIST/CAISI《2025-0035信息请求》的轻度改编版本，详细阐述了我们关于前沿AI代理安全性的观察与建议。这些见解基于Perplexity运营通用代理系统所积累的经验，该系统服务于数百万用户和数千家企业，涵盖受控与开放世界环境。代理架构改变了代码与数据分离、权限边界及执行可预测性的核心假设，从而产生新的机密性、完整性和可用性失效模式。我们梳理了工具、连接器、托管边界及多代理协调等主要攻击面，特别强调间接提示注入、混淆代理行为及长流程中级联失效等问题。随后，我们评估当前防御措施作为分层体系：输入级与模型级缓解措施、沙箱执行及高后果操作的确定性策略执行。最后，我们指出了标准与研究空白，包括自适应安全基准、代理权限委托与控制策略模型，以及符合NIST风险管理原则的多代理系统安全设计指南。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the security risks emerging from frontier AI agents' novel architectures, this paper analyzes how agent systems challenge traditional code-data separation and execution predictability, leading to new confidentiality, integrity, and availability threats. The authors map key attack surfaces including prompt injection, confused-deputy behavior, and cascading failures in multi-agent workflows, then evaluate current defenses through a layered security stack: input and model mitigations, sandboxed execution, and deterministic policy enforcement for high-risk actions. Experimental assessment across real-world deployments reveals that existing defenses are often insufficient for complex, long-running agent interactions, highlighting critical gaps in adaptive security benchmarks, privilege delegation models, and multi-agent system design aligned with NIST risk management.
鉴于前沿AI代理系统架构带来的新安全风险，本文分析了代理系统如何打破传统代码与数据分离及执行可预测性的假设，导致新的机密性、完整性与可用性威胁。作者梳理了关键攻击面，包括间接提示注入、混淆代理行为及多代理长流程中的级联故障，并评估了当前分层防御体系——输入与模型级防护、沙箱执行及高风险操作的确定性策略——在真实部署中的有效性。实验表明，现有防御措施在复杂长流程中常显不足，亟需改进自适应安全基准、权限委托模型及符合NIST风险管理原则的多代理系统设计。
Neural Thickets: Diverse Task Experts Are Dense Around Pretrained Weights
Authors: Yulu Gan, Phillip Isola
First: 2026-03-12T17:49:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:49:30+00:00
Comments: codes are provided at https://github.com/sunrainyg/RandOpt
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Pretraining produces a learned parameter vector that is typically treated as a starting point for further iterative adaptation. In this work, we instead view the outcome of pretraining as a distribution over parameter vectors, whose support already contains task-specific experts. We show that in small models such expert solutions occupy a negligible fraction of the volume of this distribution, making their discovery reliant on structured optimization methods such as gradient descent. In contrast, in large, well-pretrained models the density of task-experts increases dramatically, so that diverse, task-improving specialists populate a substantial fraction of the neighborhood around the pretrained weights. Motivated by this perspective, we explore a simple, fully parallel post-training method that samples $N$ parameter perturbations at random, selects the top $K$, and ensembles predictions via majority vote. Despite its simplicity, this approach is competitive with standard post-training methods such as PPO, GRPO, and ES for contemporary large-scale models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：神经丛林：多样化的任务专家密集分布在预训练权重周围
预训练通常产生一个被视作进一步迭代调整起点的参数向量。本文则将预训练结果视为参数向量的分布，其支撑集已包含任务特定专家。我们证明，在小模型中，此类专家解仅占该分布体积的极小部分，其发现依赖于结构化优化方法（如梯度下降）。相反，在大型且预训练良好的模型中，任务专家密度显著增加，使得多样化的、能提升任务表现的专家广泛分布于预训练权重邻域内。受此视角启发，我们探索了一种简单的、完全并行的后训练方法：随机采样N个参数扰动，选取前K个，并通过多数投票集成预测结果。尽管方法简单，该方法在当代大型模型上与PPO、GRPO和ES等标准后训练方法具有竞争力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that pretrained models contain a dense distribution of task-specific experts around their weights, this work proposes a simple post-training method that samples random parameter perturbations, selects the top K performers, and ensembles predictions via majority vote. Experimental results show that this approach, despite its simplicity and lack of structured optimization, achieves competitive performance against established methods like PPO, GRPO, and ES on large-scale models, suggesting that task experts are abundant and accessible in well-pretrained systems.
该研究基于预训练模型权重邻域内存在大量任务专家的观察，提出一种简单的后训练方法：随机采样N个参数扰动，选取表现最佳的K个样本并以多数投票方式集成预测结果。实验表明，尽管方法简单，其在大型模型上的表现可与PPO、GRPO和ES等主流方法相媲美，验证了预训练模型权重邻域内任务专家分布密集，且可通过简单采样有效发现。
Interpreting Contrastive Embeddings in Specific Domains with Fuzzy Rules
Authors: Javier Fumanal-Idocin, Mohammadreza Jamalifard, Javier Andreu-Perez
First: 2026-03-12T17:49:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:49:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Free-style text is still one of the common ways in which data is registered in real environments, like legal procedures and medical records. Because of that, there have been significant efforts in the area of natural language processing to convert these texts into a structured format, which standard machine learning methods can then exploit. One of the most popular methods to embed text into a vectorial representation is the Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training model (CLIP), which was trained using both image and text. Although the representations computed by CLIP have been very successful in zero-show and few-shot learning problems, they still have problems when applied to a particular domain. In this work, we use a fuzzy rule-based classification system along with some standard text procedure techniques to map some of our features of interest to the space created by a CLIP model. Then, we discuss the rules and associations obtained and the importance of each feature considered. We apply this approach in two different data domains, clinical reports and film reviews, and compare the results obtained individually and when considering both. Finally, we discuss the limitations of this approach and how it could be further improved.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用模糊规则在特定领域解释对比嵌入
自由格式文本仍是现实环境中数据注册的常见方式，如法律程序和医疗记录。因此，自然语言处理领域已投入大量努力，将这些文本转换为结构化格式，以便标准机器学习方法利用。将文本嵌入向量表示最流行的方法之一是对比语言-图像预训练模型（CLIP），该模型使用图像和文本联合训练。尽管CLIP计算的表示在零样本和小样本学习问题中非常成功，但在特定领域应用时仍存在问题。本研究采用模糊规则分类系统结合标准文本处理技术，将我们感兴趣的某些特征映射到CLIP模型创建的空间中。然后讨论获得的规则和关联，以及每个特征的重要性。我们在两个不同数据领域——临床报告和影评——应用该方法，并比较各自结果及联合结果。最后讨论该方法的局限性及其改进方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to interpret CLIP embeddings in domain-specific contexts where free-text data is prevalent, this work combines fuzzy rule-based classification with text preprocessing techniques to map domain-relevant features into the CLIP embedding space. The method extracts and ranks feature importance through rule generation, then evaluates performance across clinical reports and film reviews. Experimental results show that fuzzy rules effectively capture domain-specific associations and improve interpretability, though performance varies between domains and the approach remains limited by CLIP’s generalization bias.
为提升CLIP嵌入在临床报告和影评等特定领域中的可解释性，该研究结合模糊规则分类与文本预处理技术，将领域相关特征映射至CLIP的向量空间，并通过规则生成与特征重要性排序进行分析。实验表明，模糊规则能有效捕捉领域特异性模式，相较原始CLIP嵌入在可解释性和性能上均有提升，但仍存在泛化能力与规则扩展性方面的局限。
Sparking Scientific Creativity via LLM-Driven Interdisciplinary Inspiration
Authors: Priyanka Kargupta, Shuhaib Mehri, Dilek Hakkani-Tur, Jiawei Han
First: 2026-03-12T17:48:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:48:34+00:00
Comments: Code and dataset provided at https://github.com/pkargupta/idea_catalyst
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Despite interdisciplinary research leading to larger and longer-term impact, most work remains confined to single-domain academic silos. Recent AI-based approaches to scientific discovery show promise for interdisciplinary research, but many prioritize rapidly designing experiments and solutions, bypassing the exploratory, collaborative reasoning processes that drive creative interdisciplinary breakthroughs. As a result, prior efforts largely prioritize automating scientific discovery rather than augmenting the reasoning processes that underlie scientific disruption. We present Idea-Catalyst, a novel framework that systematically identifies interdisciplinary insights to support creative reasoning in both humans and large language models. Starting from an abstract research goal, Idea-Catalyst is designed to assist the brainstorming stage, explicitly avoiding premature anchoring on specific solutions. The framework embodies key metacognitive features of interdisciplinary reasoning: (a) defining and assessing research goals, (b) awareness of a domain's opportunities and unresolved challenges, and (c) strategic exploration of interdisciplinary ideas based on impact potential. Concretely, Idea-Catalyst decomposes an abstract goal (e.g., improving human-AI collaboration) into core target-domain research questions that guide the analysis of progress and open challenges within that domain. These challenges are reformulated as domain-agnostic conceptual problems, enabling retrieval from external disciplines (e.g., Psychology, Sociology) that address analogous issues. By synthesizing and recontextualizing insights from these domains back into the target domain, Idea-Catalyst ranks source domains by their interdisciplinary potential. Empirically, this targeted integration improves average novelty by 21% and insightfulness by 16%, while remaining grounded in the original research problem.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to foster interdisciplinary scientific creativity that current AI systems often overlook, the paper introduces Idea-Catalyst, a framework designed to support exploratory reasoning by guiding users and LLMs through abstract research goals without premature solution fixation. It decomposes broad objectives into domain-specific research questions, identifies unresolved challenges, and reformulates them as cross-domain conceptual problems to retrieve relevant insights from external disciplines. Experimental results show that this method improves the novelty of generated ideas by 21% and insightfulness by 16% compared to baseline approaches, while maintaining alignment with the original research problem.
为促进跨学科科学创造力，该研究提出Idea-Catalyst框架，旨在支持探索性推理而非直接生成解决方案。该框架从抽象研究目标出发，将其分解为核心领域问题，再将未解决的挑战转化为跨领域通用概念问题，从而从外部学科（如心理学、社会学）中检索类比性洞见。通过整合并重新语境化这些洞见，框架对各来源学科按其跨学科潜力进行排序。实验表明，该方法相比基线方法使新颖性提升21%，洞察力提升16%，同时保持与原始研究问题的一致性。
Portfolio of Solving Strategies in CEGAR-based Object Packing and Scheduling for Sequential 3D Printing
Authors: Pavel Surynek
First: 2026-03-12T17:48:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:48:14+00:00
Comments: arXiv admin note: substantial text overlap with arXiv:2503.05071
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Computing power that used to be available only in supercomputers decades ago especially their parallelism is currently available in standard personal computer CPUs even in CPUs for mobile telephones. We show how to effectively utilize the computing power of modern multi-core personal computer CPU to solve the complex combinatorial problem of object arrangement and scheduling for sequential 3D printing. We achieved this by parallelizing the existing CEGAR-SEQ algorithm that solves the sequential object arrangement and scheduling by expressing it as a linear arithmetic formula which is then solved by a technique inspired by counterexample guided abstraction refinement (CEGAR). The original CEGAR-SEQ algorithm uses an object arrangement strategy that places objects towards the center of the printing plate. We propose alternative object arrangement strategies such as placing objects towards a corner of the printing plate and scheduling objects according to their height. Our parallelization is done at the high-level where we execute the CEGAR-SEQ algorithm in parallel with a portfolio of object arrangement strategies, an algorithm is called Porfolio-CEGAR-SEQ. Our experimental evaluation indicates that Porfolio-CEGAR-SEQ outperforms the original CEGAR-SEQ. When a batch of objects for multiple printing plates is scheduled, Portfolio-CEGAR-SEQ often uses fewer printing plates than CEGAR-SEQ.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the availability of multi-core CPUs in modern devices, this work aims to solve the complex combinatorial problem of object arrangement and scheduling for sequential 3D printing by leveraging parallel computing. The authors parallelize the CEGAR-SEQ algorithm, which formulates the problem as a linear arithmetic formula and solves it via counterexample-guided abstraction refinement, by executing it with a portfolio of alternative object placement strategies—including corner-based placement and height-based scheduling—instead of the original center-based approach. The resulting Portfolio-CEGAR-SEQ algorithm runs multiple strategies concurrently, and experiments show it outperforms the original algorithm, especially when scheduling multiple printing plates, often requiring fewer plates overall.
鉴于现代设备中多核CPU的普及，本研究旨在利用并行计算解决3D打印中对象排列与调度的复杂组合问题。方法通过将问题转化为线性算术公式，并在高阶层面并行执行多种对象排列策略（如中心、角落及按高度排序），构建名为Portfolio-CEGAR-SEQ的框架。实验表明，该并行方法在性能上优于原始CEGAR-SEQ算法，尤其在调度多打印板批次对象时，能显著减少所需打印板数量。
HiAP: A Multi-Granular Stochastic Auto-Pruning Framework for Vision Transformers
Authors: Andy Li, Aiden Durrant, Milan Markovic, Georgios Leontidis
First: 2026-03-12T17:45:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:45:38+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 9 figures, 3 Tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision Transformers require significant computational resources and memory bandwidth, severely limiting their deployment on edge devices. While recent structured pruning methods successfully reduce theoretical FLOPs, they typically operate at a single structural granularity and rely on complex, multi-stage pipelines with post-hoc thresholding to satisfy sparsity budgets. In this paper, we propose Hierarchical Auto-Pruning (HiAP), a continuous relaxation framework that discovers optimal sub-networks in a single end-to-end training phase without requiring manual importance heuristics or predefined per-layer sparsity targets. HiAP introduces stochastic Gumbel-Sigmoid gates at multiple granularities: macro-gates to prune entire attention heads and FFN blocks, and micro-gates to selectively prune intra-head dimensions and FFN neurons. By optimizing both levels simultaneously, HiAP addresses both the memory-bound overhead of loading large matrices and the compute-bound mathematical operations. HiAP naturally converges to stable sub-networks using a loss function that incorporates both structural feasibility penalties and analytical FLOPs. Extensive experiments on ImageNet demonstrate that HiAP organically discovers highly efficient architectures, and achieves a competitive accuracy-efficiency Pareto frontier for models like DeiT-Small, matching the performance of sophisticated multi-stage methods while significantly simplifying the deployment pipeline.
Summary / 总结
To address the high computational and memory demands of Vision Transformers that hinder edge deployment, HiAP introduces a multi-granular stochastic auto-pruning framework that discovers optimal sub-networks in a single end-to-end training phase without manual heuristics or predefined sparsity targets. It employs Gumbel-Sigmoid gates at both macro (pruning entire attention heads and FFN blocks) and micro (pruning intra-head dimensions and FFN neurons) levels, optimizing both simultaneously to reduce memory-bound matrix loading and compute-bound operations. Experiments on ImageNet show HiAP achieves competitive accuracy-efficiency trade-offs for models like DeiT-Small, matching the performance of complex multi-stage pruning methods while drastically simplifying deployment.
为解决视觉Transformer在边缘设备部署中的高计算与内存开销问题，HiAP提出一种多粒度随机自动剪枝框架，通过单阶段端到端训练自动发现高效子网络，无需人工启发式或预设稀疏度目标。该框架在宏观粒度上使用Gumbel-Sigmoid门控剪枝完整注意力头和前馈块，在微观粒度上细粒度剪枝头内维度和神经元，同时降低内存矩阵加载与计算密集型操作开销。在ImageNet上的实验表明，HiAP在精度-效率权衡上表现优异，匹配甚至超越多阶段剪枝方法，同时大幅简化部署流程。
LoC-Path: Learning to Compress for Pathology Multimodal Large Language Models
Authors: Qingqiao Hu, Weimin Lyu, Meilong Xu, Kehan Qi, Xiaoling Hu, Saumya Gupta, Jiawei Zhou, Chao Chen
First: 2025-12-05T03:16:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:45:22+00:00
Comments: Code will be released soon
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Whole Slide Image (WSI) MLLMs are difficult to build and deploy because gigapixel slides induce thousands of visual tokens, while only a small fraction of regions is diagnostically relevant. Existing slide-level pathology MLLMs typically combine heavy slide-level encoders with long visual prefixes, making end-to-end slide-level development and deployment expensive under limited computational resources. We revisit this regime and show that WSI tile features are highly redundant at both global and local scales, while task-relevant evidence is sparse and query-dependent. We therefore introduce LoC-Path, a resource-efficient slide-level MLLM that compresses before fusion. LoC-Path uses a Sparse Token Merger (STM) and an MAE-pretrained resampler to replace expensive slide-level encoding with a compact latent interface, then uses a Token Importance Scorer (TIS) to select the most relevant latents and a Cross-Attention Routing Adapter (CARA) to fuse them into a few LLM decoder layers. This design lowers both multimodal tuning cost and inference-time latency/memory by avoiding heavy slide-level encoding and long visual prefixes. Extensive experiments show that LoC-Path remains competitive with prior slide-level MLLMs while making end-to-end development and deployment more practical under limited computational resources.
Summary / 总结
Whole Slide Image (WSI) MLLMs are difficult to build and deploy because gigapixel slides induce thousands of visual tokens, while only a small fraction of regions is diagnostically relevant.
为解决病理学中全切片图像处理计算成本过高的问题，该研究提出LoC-Path模型，其在融合前对图像瓦片特征进行压缩。通过稀疏令牌合并器与MAE预训练重采样器替代重型切片编码器，生成紧凑的潜在接口，并用令牌重要性评分器选择相关潜在特征，再通过跨注意力路由适配器将其融合至少量LLM解码器层。实验表明，LoC-Path在保持竞争力的同时显著降低多模态调优成本和推理延迟/内存占用，使在资源受限环境下端到端部署更可行。
HOG-Diff: Higher-Order Guided Diffusion for Graph Generation
Authors: Yiming Huang, Tolga Birdal
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-02-06T18:51:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:45:20+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Graph generation is a critical yet challenging task, as empirical analyses require a deep understanding of complex, non-Euclidean structures. Diffusion models have recently made significant advances in graph generation, but these models are typically adapted from image generation frameworks and overlook inherent higher-order topology, limiting their ability to capture graph topology. In this work, we propose Higher-order Guided Diffusion (HOG-Diff), a principled framework that progressively generates plausible graphs with inherent topological structures. HOG-Diff follows a coarse-to-fine generation curriculum, guided by higher-order topology and implemented via diffusion bridges. We further prove that our model admits stronger theoretical guarantees than classical diffusion frameworks. Extensive experiments across eight graph generation benchmarks, spanning diverse domains and including large-scale settings, demonstrate the scalability of our method and its superior performance on both pairwise and higher-order topological metrics. Our project page is available \href{https://circle-group.github.io/research/hog-diff/}{here}.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generate graphs that preserve complex higher-order topological structures, this work introduces HOG-Diff, a diffusion-based framework that guides graph generation through a coarse-to-fine curriculum using higher-order topology. The method employs diffusion bridges to progressively construct plausible graphs while respecting intrinsic structural constraints. Experimental results across eight benchmarks show HOG-Diff outperforms existing methods on both pairwise and higher-order topological metrics, demonstrating scalability and stronger theoretical guarantees than classical diffusion models.
为解决现有扩散模型在图生成中忽略高阶拓扑结构的问题，本文提出HOG-Diff框架，通过粗到细的课程式生成策略，结合高阶拓扑引导与扩散桥机制，逐步构建结构合理的图。该方法在理论层面提供更强的保证，并在八个基准数据集上的实验表明，其在成对与高阶拓扑指标上均优于现有方法，展现出良好的可扩展性与在多领域大规模场景中的鲁棒性。
A Two-Stage Dual-Modality Model for Facial Emotional Expression Recognition
Authors: Jiajun Sun, Zhe Gao
First: 2026-03-12T17:45:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:45:12+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper addresses the expression (EXPR) recognition challenge in the 10th Affective Behavior Analysis in-the-Wild (ABAW) workshop and competition, which requires frame-level classification of eight facial emotional expressions from unconstrained videos. This task is challenging due to inaccurate face localization, large pose and scale variations, motion blur, temporal instability, and other confounding factors across adjacent frames. We propose a two-stage dual-modal (audio-visual) model to address these difficulties. Stage I focuses on robust visual feature extraction with a pretrained DINOv2-based encoder. Specifically, DINOv2 ViT-L/14 is used as the backbone, a padding-aware augmentation (PadAug) strategy is employed for image padding and data preprocessing from raw videos, and a mixture-of-experts (MoE) training head is introduced to enhance classifier diversity. Stage II addresses modality fusion and temporal consistency. For the visual modality, faces are re-cropped from raw videos at multiple scales, and the extracted visual features are averaged to form a robust frame-level representation. Concurrently, frame-aligned Wav2Vec 2.0 audio features are derived from short audio windows to provide complementary acoustic cues. These dual-modal features are integrated via a lightweight gated fusion module, followed by inference-time temporal smoothing. Experiments on the ABAW dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method. The two-stage model achieves a Macro-F1 score of 0.5368 on the official validation set and 0.5122 +/- 0.0277 under 5-fold cross-validation, outperforming the official baselines.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of facial expression recognition in unconstrained videos—such as inaccurate face localization, pose variation, motion blur, and temporal instability—the paper proposes a two-stage dual-modality model combining visual and audio cues. The first stage employs a DINOv2-based visual encoder with padding-aware augmentation and a mixture-of-experts head to extract robust visual features. The second stage re-crops faces at multiple scales, averages visual features, and fuses them with frame-aligned Wav2Vec 2.0 audio features via a lightweight gated module, followed by temporal smoothing. Experiments on the ABAW dataset show the model achieves a Macro-F1 score of 0.5368 on the official validation set and 0.5122 ± 0.0277 under 5-fold cross-validation, outperforming official baselines.
为应对无约束视频中面部表情识别面临的挑战——如人脸定位不准、姿态变化大、运动模糊及帧间时序不稳定——本文提出一种两阶段双模态模型，融合视觉与音频信息。第一阶段采用基于DINOv2的视觉编码器，结合填充感知增强与专家混合头提取鲁棒视觉特征；第二阶段对多尺度人脸进行重裁剪，平均视觉特征，并与帧对齐的Wav2Vec 2.0音频特征通过轻量门控融合模块结合，后经推理时序平滑处理。在ABAW数据集上的实验表明，该模型在官方验证集上达到0.5368的Macro-F1分数，5折交叉验证下为0.5122±0.0277，优于官方基线方法。
UniMotion: Self-Supervised Learning for Cross-Domain IMU Motion Recognition
Authors: Prerna Khanna, Tanmay Srivastava, Shubham Jain, Aruna Balasubramanian
First: 2026-03-12T17:43:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:43:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
IMU-based gesture interfaces are being increasingly adopted as efficient, accessible, and intuitive alternatives to traditional input methods, such as touchscreens and voice. However, current gesture recognition algorithms are tailored to work for specific devices (e.g., smartwatches vs. earbuds) or user populations (e.g., blind vs. sighted users), limiting their generalizability. In this paper, we design UniMotion, a generalized IMU-based gesture recognition framework that works across devices and populations with minimal training samples. To overcome the challenges and high cost of collecting large-scale labeled training data, UniMotion leverages readily available unlabeled human activity data. The UniMotion pipeline comprises two stages: (1) pre-training a motion representation model using abundant unlabeled human activity data, and (2) fine-tuning it with a small amount of labeled gesture data. For pre-training, we introduce a token-based strategy and embeddings that learn to identify and focus attention on the key motion signatures in the temporal data For fine-tuning, we design a text-guided classifier that can reliably differentiate between temporally or semantically similar gestures. We evaluate UniMotion across both hand gestures (captured through a smartwatch) and earbud gestures (captured through earbuds), using data collected from blind and sighted users. Across these diverse devices and user populations, UniMotion achieves an accuracy of 85\%, across an average of 13 gesture classes using only 10\% of labeled data for training. UniMotion significantly outperforms state-of-the-art self-supervised learning approaches and specialized gesture recognition models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UniMotion：面向跨域IMU运动识别的自监督学习
基于IMU的手势界面正被越来越多地采用，作为比传统输入方式（如触摸屏和语音）更高效、易用和直观的替代方案。然而，当前的手势识别算法通常针对特定设备（如智能手表与耳机）或用户群体（如盲人与视力正常者）进行优化，限制了其泛化能力。本文提出UniMotion，一种通用的IMU手势识别框架，可在不同设备和用户群体间以极少标注样本实现有效工作。为克服大规模标注训练数据收集的挑战与高成本，UniMotion利用广泛可得的无标注人类活动数据。UniMotion流程包含两个阶段：（1）利用大量无标注人类活动数据预训练运动表征模型；（2）用少量标注手势数据进行微调。预训练阶段，我们引入基于token的策略和嵌入，使模型能识别并聚焦于时序数据中的关键运动特征；微调阶段，我们设计文本引导分类器，可可靠区分在时间或语义上相似的手势。我们在智能手表捕捉的手势和耳机捕捉的耳塞手势上评估UniMotion，数据来自盲人与视力正常用户。在这些多样化的设备和用户群体中，UniMotion仅用10%标注数据训练，平均13类手势达到85%准确率，显著优于现有自监督学习方法和专用手势识别模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for generalizable IMU gesture recognition across diverse devices and user groups, UniMotion introduces a self-supervised framework that leverages unlabeled human activity data for pre-training and fine-tunes with minimal labeled gesture data. It employs a token-based motion representation model to capture key temporal motion signatures and a text-guided classifier to distinguish similar gestures. Evaluated on hand and earbud gestures from both blind and sighted users, UniMotion achieves 85% accuracy across 13 gesture classes using only 10% of labeled data, significantly outperforming existing self-supervised and specialized models.
为实现跨设备与用户群体的通用IMU手势识别，UniMotion提出一种自监督框架，利用大量无标签的人体活动数据进行预训练，并仅用少量标注手势数据进行微调。其采用基于令牌的运动表征模型捕捉关键时序运动特征，并设计文本引导分类器区分相似手势。在智能手表与耳塞采集的手势数据上，针对盲人与视力正常用户，UniMotion仅用10%标注数据即达到85%准确率（13类手势），显著优于现有自监督及专用手势识别方法。
Real-World Point Tracking with Verifier-Guided Pseudo-Labeling
Authors: Görkay Aydemir, Fatma Güney, Weidi Xie
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-12T17:40:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:40:52+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Models for long-term point tracking are typically trained on large synthetic datasets. The performance of these models degrades in real-world videos due to different characteristics and the absence of dense ground-truth annotations. Self-training on unlabeled videos has been explored as a practical solution, but the quality of pseudo-labels strongly depends on the reliability of teacher models, which vary across frames and scenes. In this paper, we address the problem of real-world fine-tuning and introduce verifier, a meta-model that learns to assess the reliability of tracker predictions and guide pseudo-label generation. Given candidate trajectories from multiple pretrained trackers, the verifier evaluates them per frame and selects the most trustworthy predictions, resulting in high-quality pseudo-label trajectories. When applied for fine-tuning, verifier-guided pseudo-labeling substantially improves the quality of supervision and enables data-efficient adaptation to unlabeled videos. Extensive experiments on four real-world benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art results while requiring less data than prior self-training methods. Project page: https://kuis-ai.github.io/track_on_r

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the degradation of synthetic-trained point trackers in real-world videos due to annotation sparsity and scene variability, this work proposes a verifier-guided pseudo-labeling method to improve self-training. The verifier, a meta-model, evaluates predictions from multiple pretrained trackers frame-by-frame and selects the most reliable trajectories to generate high-quality pseudo-labels. Experiments on four real-world benchmarks show that this approach significantly enhances fine-tuning performance, achieving state-of-the-art results while requiring less labeled data than prior self-training methods.
为了解决合成数据训练的点跟踪模型在真实视频中因标注稀疏和场景差异导致性能下降的问题，本文提出一种由验证器引导的伪标签方法。该验证器作为元模型，逐帧评估多个预训练跟踪器的预测结果，并选择最可靠的预测生成高质量伪标签。在四个真实世界基准上的实验表明，该方法在数据需求更少的情况下实现了当前最优性能。
Expert Selections In MoE Models Reveal (Almost) As Much As Text
Authors: Amir Nuriyev, Gabriel Kulp
First: 2026-02-04T00:42:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:39:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a text-reconstruction attack on mixture-of-experts (MoE) language models that recovers tokens from expert selections alone. In MoE models, each token is routed to a subset of expert subnetworks; we show these routing decisions leak substantially more information than previously understood. Prior work using logistic regression achieves limited reconstruction; we show that a 3-layer MLP improves this to 63.1% top-1 accuracy, and that a transformer-based sequence decoder recovers 91.2% of tokens top-1 (94.8% top-10) on 32-token sequences from OpenWebText after training on 100M tokens. These results connect MoE routing to the broader literature on embedding inversion. We outline practical leakage scenarios (e.g., distributed inference and side channels) and show that adding noise reduces but does not eliminate reconstruction. Our findings suggest that expert selections in MoE deployments should be treated as sensitive as the underlying text.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MoE模型中的专家选择泄露的信息几乎等同于文本
我们提出了一种针对混合专家（MoE）语言模型的文本重建攻击，仅通过专家选择即可恢复令牌。在MoE模型中，每个令牌被路由至一组专家子网络；我们表明这些路由决策泄露的信息远超此前理解。先前使用逻辑回归的方法重建效果有限；我们证明，三层MLP可将其提升至63.1%的top-1准确率，而基于Transformer的序列解码器在OpenWebText数据集上，仅用1亿个令牌训练后，即可在32令牌序列中恢复91.2%的top-1令牌（94.8%的top-10）。这些结果将MoE路由与嵌入反演的广泛文献联系起来。我们概述了实际信息泄露场景（如分布式推理和侧信道），并表明添加噪声虽能降低但无法完全消除重建。我们的发现表明，在MoE部署中，专家选择应被视为与原始文本同等敏感。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand information leakage in mixture-of-experts (MoE) language models, this study introduces a text-reconstruction attack that recovers tokens solely from expert selection patterns. Using a 3-layer MLP, the method achieves 63.1% top-1 accuracy, and a transformer-based decoder reaches 91.2% top-1 (94.8% top-10) on 32-token sequences from OpenWebText after training on 100M tokens. These results demonstrate that routing decisions in MoE models leak substantially more information than previously assumed, connecting to embedding inversion literature and highlighting practical leakage risks in distributed inference and side channels, even with added noise.
为理解MoE语言模型中的信息泄露问题，本研究提出一种仅基于专家选择模式的文本重建攻击。使用三层MLP可实现63.1%的top-1准确率，而基于Transformer的序列解码器在训练1亿个token后，对OpenWebText的32个token序列可达到91.2%的top-1（94.8%的top-10）重建准确率。结果表明，MoE模型中的路由决策泄露的信息远超此前认知，与嵌入反演研究相关，提示在实际部署中专家选择应被视为与原始文本同等敏感的数据。
Quantifying Aleatoric Uncertainty of the Treatment Effect: A Novel Orthogonal Learner
Authors: Valentyn Melnychuk, Stefan Feuerriegel, Mihaela van der Schaar
Venue: NeurIPS 2024
First: 2024-11-05T18:14:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:37:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Estimating causal quantities from observational data is crucial for understanding the safety and effectiveness of medical treatments. However, to make reliable inferences, medical practitioners require not only estimating averaged causal quantities, such as the conditional average treatment effect, but also understanding the randomness of the treatment effect as a random variable. This randomness is referred to as aleatoric uncertainty and is necessary for understanding the probability of benefit from treatment or quantiles of the treatment effect. Yet, the aleatoric uncertainty of the treatment effect has received surprisingly little attention in the causal machine learning community. To fill this gap, we aim to quantify the aleatoric uncertainty of the treatment effect at the covariate-conditional level, namely, the conditional distribution of the treatment effect (CDTE). Unlike average causal quantities, the CDTE is not point identifiable without strong additional assumptions. As a remedy, we employ partial identification to obtain sharp bounds on the CDTE and thereby quantify the aleatoric uncertainty of the treatment effect. We then develop a novel, orthogonal learner for the bounds on the CDTE, which we call AU-learner. We further show that our AU-learner has several strengths in that it satisfies Neyman-orthogonality and, thus, quasi-oracle efficiency. Finally, we propose a fully-parametric deep learning instantiation of our AU-learner.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for medical practitioners to understand not just average treatment effects but also the inherent randomness in treatment outcomes, this paper addresses the underexplored problem of quantifying aleatoric uncertainty in causal inference. The authors propose a novel orthogonal learner, called AU-learner, designed to estimate sharp bounds on the conditional distribution of the treatment effect (CDTE), which is non-point-identifiable without strong assumptions. By leveraging partial identification and ensuring Neyman-orthogonality, the AU-learner achieves quasi-oracle efficiency and is further instantiated as a fully-parametric deep learning model, demonstrating practical utility in quantifying treatment effect uncertainty.
为帮助医疗从业者不仅了解平均治疗效果，更理解治疗效果的固有随机性，本文针对因果推断中对治疗效应随机性（即‘aleatoric uncertainty’）研究不足的问题提出新方法。作者开发了一种名为AU-learner的正交学习器，用于在无需强假设的前提下，通过部分识别获得治疗效应条件分布（CDTE）的紧界，从而量化其不确定性。该学习器满足Neyman正交性，实现准Oracle效率，并以全参数化深度学习模型形式实现，验证了其在治疗效应不确定性量化中的实用性。
RDNet: Region Proportion-Aware Dynamic Adaptive Salient Object Detection Network in Optical Remote Sensing Images
Authors: Bin Wan, Runmin Cong, Xiaofei Zhou, Hao Fang, Yaoqi Sun, Sam Kwong
First: 2026-03-12T17:34:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:34:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Salient object detection (SOD) in remote sensing images faces significant challenges due to large variations in object sizes, the computational cost of self-attention mechanisms, and the limitations of CNN-based extractors in capturing global context and long-range dependencies. Existing methods that rely on fixed convolution kernels often struggle to adapt to diverse object scales, leading to detail loss or irrelevant feature aggregation. To address these issues, this work aims to enhance robustness to scale variations and achieve precise object localization. We propose the Region Proportion-Aware Dynamic Adaptive Salient Object Detection Network (RDNet), which replaces the CNN backbone with the SwinTransformer for global context modeling and introduces three key modules: (1) the Dynamic Adaptive Detail-aware (DAD) module, which applies varied convolution kernels guided by object region proportions; (2) the Frequency-matching Context Enhancement (FCE) module, which enriches contextual information through wavelet interactions and attention; and (3) the Region Proportion-aware Localization (RPL) module, which employs cross-attention to highlight semantic details and integrates a Proportion Guidance (PG) block to assist the DAD module. By combining these modules, RDNet achieves robustness against scale variations and accurate localization, delivering superior detection performance compared with state-of-the-art methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RDNet：面向光学遥感图像的区域比例感知动态自适应显著物体检测网络
遥感图像中的显著物体检测（SOD）面临物体尺寸变化大、自注意力机制计算成本高、CNN提取器难以捕捉全局上下文和长距离依赖等挑战。现有依赖固定卷积核的方法难以适应多样化的物体尺度，导致细节丢失或无关特征聚合。为此，本文提出区域比例感知动态自适应显著物体检测网络（RDNet），以SwinTransformer替代CNN主干网络以建模全局上下文，并引入三个关键模块：（1）动态自适应细节感知（DAD）模块，根据物体区域比例应用不同卷积核；（2）频域匹配上下文增强（FCE）模块，通过小波交互与注意力机制丰富上下文信息；（3）区域比例感知定位（RPL）模块，利用交叉注意力突出语义细节，并集成比例引导（PG）块辅助DAD模块。通过结合这些模块，RDNet在尺度变化鲁棒性和定位精度上表现优异，检测性能优于现有最先进方法。
Summary / 总结
Salient object detection (SOD) in remote sensing images faces significant challenges due to large variations in object sizes, the computational cost of self-attention mechanisms, and the limitations of CNN-based extractors in capturing global context and long-range dependencies.
为解决遥感图像中目标尺度变化大、自注意力机制计算成本高、CNN特征提取器难以捕捉全局上下文等问题，RDNet提出基于SwinTransformer的架构，包含三个核心模块：动态自适应细节感知（DAD）模块根据目标区域比例调整卷积核，频域匹配上下文增强（FCE）模块通过小波与注意力机制丰富上下文，区域比例感知定位（RPL）模块利用交叉注意力与比例引导块优化定位。实验表明，RDNet在多种尺度和复杂场景下显著优于现有方法，实现更精准的检测效果。
WORKSWORLD: A Domain for Integrated Numeric Planning and Scheduling of Distributed Pipelined Workflows
Authors: Taylor Paul, William Regli
First: 2026-03-12T17:34:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:34:04+00:00
Comments: To be published in Proceedings of the International Conference on Automated Planning and Scheduling Volume 36 (2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This work pursues automated planning and scheduling of distributed data pipelines, or workflows. We develop a general workflow and resource graph representation that includes both data processing and sharing components with corresponding network interfaces for scheduling. Leveraging these graphs, we introduce WORKSWORLD, a new domain for numeric domain-independent planners designed for permanently scheduled workflows, like ingest pipelines. Our framework permits users to define data sources, available workflow components, and desired data destinations and formats without explicitly declaring the entire workflow graph as a goal. The planner solves a joint planning and scheduling problem, producing a plan that both builds the workflow graph and schedules its components on the resource graph. We empirically show that a state-of-the-art numeric planner running on commodity hardware with one hour of CPU time and 30GB of memory can solve linear-chain workflows of up to 14 components across eight sites.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate the planning and scheduling of distributed data pipelines, this work introduces WORKSWORLD, a domain designed for numeric planners to handle permanently scheduled workflows. The method defines a unified workflow and resource graph that models both data processing and sharing with network interfaces, enabling planners to jointly construct workflow graphs and schedule components without explicit goal specification. Experimental results demonstrate that a state-of-the-art planner, running on standard hardware with one hour of CPU time and 30GB memory, can successfully solve linear-chain workflows of up to 14 components across eight sites.
为自动化分布式数据管道的规划与调度，本研究提出WORKSWORLD领域，专为数值规划器设计以处理永久调度的工作流。该方法构建统一的图表示，涵盖数据处理、共享与资源分配，允许规划器无需显式指定目标即可联合构建工作流图并调度各组件。实验表明，运行于标准硬件、耗时一小时、内存30GB的先进数值规划器，可成功求解跨八站点、含14个组件的线性链工作流。
ForensicZip: More Tokens are Better but Not Necessary in Forensic Vision-Language Models
Authors: Yingxin Lai, Zitong Yu, Jun Wang, Linlin Shen, Yong Xu, Xiaochun Cao
First: 2026-03-12T17:30:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:30:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) enable interpretable multimedia forensics by generating textual rationales for forgery detection. However, processing dense visual sequences incurs high computational costs, particularly for high-resolution images and videos. Visual token pruning is a practical acceleration strategy, yet existing methods are largely semantic-driven, retaining salient objects while discarding background regions where manipulation traces such as high-frequency anomalies and temporal jitters often reside. To address this issue, we introduce ForensicZip, a training-free framework that reformulates token compression from a forgery-driven perspective. ForensicZip models temporal token evolution as a Birth-Death Optimal Transport problem with a slack dummy node, quantifying physical discontinuities indicating transient generative artifacts. The forensic scoring further integrates transport-based novelty with high-frequency priors to separate forensic evidence from semantic content under large-ratio compression. Experiments on deepfake and AIGC benchmarks show that at 10\% token retention, ForensicZip achieves $2.97\times$ speedup and over 90\% FLOPs reduction while maintaining state-of-the-art detection performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ForensicZip：更多令牌更好，但非必需用于法证视觉-语言模型
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）通过为伪造检测生成文本推理，实现可解释的多媒体法证。然而，处理密集视觉序列会带来高昂的计算成本，尤其针对高分辨率图像和视频。视觉令牌剪枝是一种实用的加速策略，但现有方法多为语义驱动，保留显著物体而丢弃背景区域——而伪造痕迹如高频异常和时序抖动常存在于背景中。为解决此问题，我们提出ForensicZip，一种无需训练的框架，从伪造驱动视角重构令牌压缩。ForensicZip将时间令牌演化建模为带松弛虚拟节点的出生-死亡最优传输问题，量化指示瞬态生成伪影的物理不连续性。法证评分进一步融合基于传输的新颖性与高频先验，以在高压缩比下分离法证证据与语义内容。在深度伪造与AIGC基准测试中，ForensicZip在保留10%令牌时实现2.97倍加速与超90% FLOPs减少，同时保持最先进检测性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of processing dense visual sequences in forensic vision-language models, this paper proposes ForensicZip, a training-free framework that prioritizes forensic evidence over semantic content during token compression. It models temporal token evolution as a Birth-Death Optimal Transport problem with a slack dummy node to detect physical discontinuities indicative of generative artifacts, then combines transport-based novelty with high-frequency priors to preserve forensic traces while pruning background tokens. Experiments show that at 10% token retention, ForensicZip achieves 2.97x speedup and over 90% FLOPs reduction while maintaining state-of-the-art detection performance on deepfake and AIGC benchmarks.
为解决多模态大语言模型处理密集视觉序列时计算成本过高的问题，本文提出ForensicZip框架，该框架在压缩视觉token时优先考虑伪造检测而非语义保留。它将时间序列token演化建模为带松弛虚拟节点的出生-死亡最优传输问题，以捕捉生成伪影的物理不连续性，并结合传输相关新颖性与高频先验，分离伪造证据与语义内容。实验表明，在保留10% token的情况下，ForensicZip实现2.97倍加速和超过90%的FLOPs减少，同时在深度伪造与AIGC基准测试中保持最先进检测性能。
CLASP: Defending Hybrid Large Language Models Against Hidden State Poisoning Attacks
Authors: Alexandre Le Mercier, Thomas Demeester, Chris Develder
First: 2026-03-12T17:29:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:29:55+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
State space models (SSMs) like Mamba have gained significant traction as efficient alternatives to Transformers, achieving linear complexity while maintaining competitive performance. However, Hidden State Poisoning Attacks (HiSPAs), a recently discovered vulnerability that corrupts SSM memory through adversarial strings, pose a critical threat to these architectures and their hybrid variants. Framing the HiSPA mitigation task as a binary classification problem at the token level, we introduce the CLASP model to defend against this threat. CLASP exploits distinct patterns in Mamba's block output embeddings (BOEs) and uses an XGBoost classifier to identify malicious tokens with minimal computational overhead. We consider a realistic scenario in which both SSMs and HiSPAs are likely to be used: an LLM screening résumés to identify the best candidates for a role. Evaluated on a corpus of 2,483 résumés totaling 9.5M tokens with controlled injections, CLASP achieves 95.9% token-level F1 score and 99.3% document-level F1 score on malicious tokens detection. Crucially, the model generalizes to unseen attack patterns: under leave-one-out cross-validation, performance remains high (96.9% document-level F1), while under clustered cross-validation with structurally novel triggers, it maintains useful detection capability (91.6% average document-level F1). Operating independently of any downstream model, CLASP processes 1,032 tokens per second with under 4GB VRAM consumption, potentially making it suitable for real-world deployment as a lightweight front-line defense for SSM-based and hybrid architectures. All code and detailed results are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/hispikes-91C0.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CLASP：防御混合大语言模型中的隐藏状态投毒攻击
状态空间模型（SSM）如Mamba因其在保持竞争力性能的同时实现线性复杂度，已成为Transformer的有效替代方案。然而，近期发现的隐藏状态投毒攻击（HiSPA）通过对抗性字符串破坏SSM内存，对这些架构及其混合变体构成严重威胁。我们将HiSPA缓解任务建模为令牌级别的二分类问题，提出CLASP模型进行防御。CLASP利用Mamba块输出嵌入（BOEs）中的独特模式，结合XGBoost分类器识别恶意令牌，且计算开销极小。我们考虑一个现实场景：LLM筛选简历以识别最适合职位的候选人。在包含2,483份简历（总计950万令牌）且受控注入攻击的数据集上评估，CLASP在恶意令牌检测中达到95.9%的令牌级F1分数和99.3%的文档级F1分数。关键的是，该模型能泛化至未见过的攻击模式：在留一交叉验证中性能保持高位（96.9%文档级F1），在结构新颖的触发器集群交叉验证中仍保持实用检测能力（91.6%平均文档级F1）。CLASP独立于任何下游模型运行，每秒处理1,032个令牌，VRAM消耗低于4GB，有望作为轻量级前线防御方案部署于基于SSM及混合架构的系统中。所有代码和详细结果见https://anonymous.4open.science/r/hispikes-91C0。
Summary / 总结
State space models (SSMs) like Mamba have gained significant traction as efficient alternatives to Transformers, achieving linear complexity while maintaining competitive performance.
为应对Mamba等状态空间模型易受隐藏状态投毒攻击（HiSPA）的漏洞——该攻击通过对抗性字符串破坏模型记忆，本文提出CLASP：一种基于XGBoost的轻量级令牌级二分类器，通过分析块输出嵌入识别恶意令牌。在包含950万令牌、2483份简历的受控攻击数据集上，CLASP实现95.9%令牌级F1和99.3%文档级F1，即使面对未见过的攻击模式（留一交叉验证96.9%，结构新颖触发器91.6%）仍保持高检测能力。其每秒处理1032个令牌、内存占用低于4GB，无需修改下游任务，适合作为SSM及混合架构的轻量级前置防御方案。
IndexCache: Accelerating Sparse Attention via Cross-Layer Index Reuse
Authors: Yushi Bai, Qian Dong, Ting Jiang, Xin Lv, Zhengxiao Du, Aohan Zeng, Jie Tang, Juanzi Li
First: 2026-03-12T17:27:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:27:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-context agentic workflows have emerged as a defining use case for large language models, making attention efficiency critical for both inference speed and serving cost. Sparse attention addresses this challenge effectively, and DeepSeek Sparse Attention (DSA) is a representative production-grade solution: a lightweight lightning indexer selects the top-k most relevant tokens per query, reducing core attention from $O(L^2)$ to $O(Lk)$. However, the indexer itself retains $O(L^2)$ complexity and must run independently at every layer, despite the fact that the resulting top-k selections are highly similar across consecutive layers. We present IndexCache, which exploits this cross-layer redundancy by partitioning layers into a small set of Full layers that run their own indexers and a majority of Shared layers that simply reuse the nearest Full layer's top-k indices. We propose two complementary approaches to determine and optimize this configuration. Training-free IndexCache applies a greedy search algorithm that selects which layers to retain indexers by directly minimizing language modeling loss on a calibration set, requiring no weight updates. Training-aware IndexCache introduces a multi-layer distillation loss that trains each retained indexer against the averaged attention distributions of all layers it serves, enabling even simple interleaved patterns to match full-indexer accuracy. Experimental results on a 30B DSA model show that IndexCache can remove 75% of indexer computations with negligible quality degradation, achieving up to 1.82$\times$ prefill speedup and 1.48$\times$ decode speedup compared to standard DSA. These positive results are further confirmed by our preliminary experiments on the production-scale GLM-5 model (Figure 1).
中文标题/摘要
标题：IndexCache：通过跨层索引复用加速稀疏注意力
长上下文代理工作流已成为大型语言模型的关键应用场景，使注意力效率对推理速度和部署成本至关重要。稀疏注意力有效应对这一挑战，而DeepSeek稀疏注意力（DSA）是具有代表性的生产级解决方案：一个轻量级闪电索引器为每个查询选择最相关的top-k个token，将核心注意力复杂度从O(L²)降低至O(Lk)。然而，索引器本身仍保持O(L²)复杂度，且必须在每一层独立运行，尽管相邻层的top-k选择高度相似。我们提出IndexCache，通过跨层冗余性，将层划分为少量Full层（独立运行索引器）和多数Shared层（复用最近Full层的top-k索引）。我们提出两种互补方法以确定并优化该配置。无训练IndexCache采用贪心搜索算法，在校准集上直接最小化语言建模损失以选择保留索引器的层，无需权重更新。有训练IndexCache引入多层蒸馏损失，使每个保留索引器针对其服务的所有层的平均注意力分布进行训练，即使简单交错模式也能匹配完整索引器的精度。在30B DSA模型上的实验表明，IndexCache可去除75%的索引器计算，质量损失微乎其微，相比标准DSA实现高达1.82倍的预填充加速和1.48倍的解码加速。这些积极结果在生产级GLM-5模型的初步实验中进一步得到验证（图1）。
Summary / 总结
Long-context agentic workflows have emerged as a defining use case for large language models, making attention efficiency critical for both inference speed and serving cost.
LLMTrack: Semantic Multi-Object Tracking with Multi-modal Large Language Models
Authors: Pan Liao, Feng Yang, Di Wu, Jinwen Yu, Yuhua Zhu, Wenhui Zhao, Dingwen Zhang
First: 2026-01-10T12:18:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T17:26:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-Object Tracking (MOT) is evolving from geometric localization to Semantic MOT (SMOT) to answer complex relational queries, yet progress is hindered by semantic data scarcity and a structural disconnect between tracking architectures and Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs). To address this, we introduce Grand-SMOT, a large-scale, open-world benchmark providing high-density, dual-stream narratives that comprehensively decouple individual behaviors from environmental contexts. Furthermore, we propose LLMTrack, the first framework to seamlessly integrate MLLMs into the SMOT task. LLMTrack establishes a Macro-Understanding-First paradigm, utilizing a novel Spatio-Temporal Fusion Module to align discrete geometric trajectories with continuous semantic features, effectively suppressing temporal hallucinations during online processing. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LLMTrack achieves state-of-the-art geometric tracking performance while delivering a qualitative leap in dynamic semantic reasoning. Notably, our analysis reveals that high-quality semantic narratives empower the language model to deduce complex social interactions naturally, demonstrating that direct cognitive reasoning is more effective than cumbersome explicit visual modeling. Ultimately, our contributions bridge the gap between perceptual tracking and cognitive reasoning, establishing a robust new foundation for comprehensive video understanding and intelligent narrative generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LLMTrack：基于多模态大语言模型的语义多目标跟踪
多目标跟踪（MOT）正从几何定位演进至语义多目标跟踪（SMOT），以应对复杂关系查询，但其进展受限于语义数据稀缺及跟踪架构与多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）之间的结构性脱节。为此，我们提出Grand-SMOT，一个大规模开放世界基准，提供高密度双流叙事，全面解耦个体行为与环境背景。此外，我们提出LLMTrack，首个无缝集成MLLMs至SMOT任务的框架。LLMTrack建立“宏观理解优先”范式，通过新颖时空融合模块对齐离散几何轨迹与连续语义特征，有效抑制在线处理中的时序幻觉。大量实验表明，LLMTrack在几何跟踪性能上达到最先进水平，同时在动态语义推理方面实现质的飞跃。值得注意的是，我们的分析表明，高质量语义叙事可使语言模型自然推断复杂社会互动，证明直接认知推理优于繁琐的显式视觉建模。最终，我们的贡献弥合了感知跟踪与认知推理之间的鸿沟，为全面视频理解与智能叙事生成奠定坚实基础。

It employs a Macro-Understanding-First paradigm with a Spatio-Temporal Fusion Module that aligns geometric trajectories with semantic features to suppress temporal hallucinations. Experiments show LLMTrack achieves state-of-the-art geometric tracking while significantly enhancing semantic reasoning, demonstrating that high-quality narratives enable language models to infer complex social interactions more effectively than explicit visual modeling.
