为解决现有静态多语言基准无法准确评估大语言模型实际多语言能力的问题，作者构建了两个新的功能性基准：CL-GSM Symbolic 和 CL-IFEval，并将其翻译成五种语言（法语、西班牙语、印地语、阿拉伯语、约鲁巴语），以反映不同NLP资源水平。实验结果表明，CL-GSM Symbolic 在英语、法语和西班牙语中分别比M-GSM高出24%、17%和18%，而CL-IFEval与M-MMLU的性能差距仅为0.5%至3%，显示出更强的功能对齐能力。此外，模型在阿拉伯语和英语中的表现最为稳定，跨评估迭代的鲁棒性显著优于其他语言。
TopoBench: Benchmarking LLMs on Hard Topological Reasoning
Authors: Mayug Maniparambil, Nils Hoehing, Janak Kapuriya, Arjun Karuvally, Ellen Rushe, Anthony Ventresque, Noel O'Connor, Fergal Reid
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-03-12T16:37:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:37:21+00:00
Comments: Accepted, Workshop on Logical Reasoning of Large Language Models at ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Solving topological grid puzzles requires reasoning over global spatial invariants such as connectivity, loop closure, and region symmetry and remains challenging for even the most powerful large language models (LLMs). To study these abilities under controlled settings, we introduce TopoBench, a benchmark of six puzzle families across three difficulty levels. We evaluate strong reasoning LLMs on TopoBench and find that even frontier models solve fewer than one quarter of hard instances, with two families nearly unsolved. To investigate whether these failures stem from reasoning limitations or from difficulty extracting and maintaining spatial constraints, we annotate 750 chain of thought traces with an error taxonomy that surfaces four candidate causal failure modes, then test them with targeted interventions simulating each error type. These interventions show that certain error patterns like premature commitment and constraint forgetting have a direct impact on the ability to solve the puzzle, while repeated reasoning is a benign effect of search. Finally we study mitigation strategies including prompt guidance, cell-aligned grid representations and tool-based constraint checking, finding that the bottleneck lies in extracting constraints from spatial representations and not in reasoning over them. Code and data are available at github.com/mayug/topobench-benchmark.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty LLMs face in solving topological grid puzzles that require global spatial reasoning, the authors introduce TopoBench, a benchmark with six puzzle families across three difficulty levels. They evaluate frontier LLMs and find that even the strongest models solve fewer than 25% of hard instances, with two families nearly unsolvable. To diagnose failure causes, they annotate 750 chain-of-thought traces with an error taxonomy, identifying four failure modes, and test interventions simulating each. Results show that premature commitment and constraint forgetting significantly impair performance, while repeated reasoning is harmless. Mitigation strategies like prompt guidance and grid representations improve results, but the core bottleneck remains extracting spatial constraints, not reasoning over them.
为研究大语言模型在需要全局空间推理的拓扑网格谜题上的能力，作者构建了TopoBench基准，包含六个谜题家族和三个难度等级。评估结果显示，即使前沿模型在高难度实例上的正确率也低于25%，其中两个家族几乎无法解决。为诊断失败原因，作者对750条思维链进行标注并划分错误类型，识别出四种失败模式，并通过针对性干预实验验证。结果表明，过早承诺和约束遗忘显著影响解题能力，而重复推理无害。缓解策略如提示引导和网格对齐表示可提升表现，但核心瓶颈仍在于从空间表示中提取约束，而非推理过程本身。
Lifelong Imitation Learning with Multimodal Latent Replay and Incremental Adjustment
Authors: Fanqi Yu, Matteo Tiezzi, Tommaso Apicella, Cigdem Beyan, Vittorio Murino
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-11T16:13:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:36:57+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We introduce a lifelong imitation learning framework that enables continual policy refinement across sequential tasks under realistic memory and data constraints. Our approach departs from conventional experience replay by operating entirely in a multimodal latent space, where compact representations of visual, linguistic, and robot's state information are stored and reused to support future learning. To further stabilize adaptation, we introduce an incremental feature adjustment mechanism that regularizes the evolution of task embeddings through an angular margin constraint, preserving inter-task distinctiveness. Our method establishes a new state of the art in the LIBERO benchmarks, achieving 10-17 point gains in AUC and up to 65% less forgetting compared to previous leading methods. Ablation studies confirm the effectiveness of each component, showing consistent gains over alternative strategies. The code is available at: https://github.com/yfqi/lifelong_mlr_ifa.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for continual policy improvement in lifelong imitation learning under memory and data constraints, this work proposes a framework that operates in a multimodal latent space to store and reuse compact representations of visual, linguistic, and robotic state data. To stabilize adaptation across tasks, it introduces an incremental feature adjustment mechanism that enforces angular margin constraints on task embeddings to preserve distinctiveness. Experimental results on LIBERO benchmarks show the method achieves 10-17 point gains in AUC and up to 65% less forgetting compared to prior state-of-the-art approaches, with ablation studies confirming the individual contributions of each component.
为了解决在记忆和数据受限条件下持续改进策略的需求，该研究提出一种在多模态潜在空间中存储和重用视觉、语言及机器人状态紧凑表征的框架。为稳定跨任务适应，引入增量特征调整机制，通过角间距约束调节任务嵌入的演化，保持任务间区分性。在LIBERO基准测试中，该方法相较现有最先进方法在AUC上提升10-17分，遗忘率降低高达65%，消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。
On the Theoretical Limitations of Embedding-Based Retrieval
Authors: Orion Weller, Michael Boratko, Iftekhar Naim, Jinhyuk Lee
Venue: ICLR
First: 2025-08-28T17:43:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:35:48+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICLR'26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vector embeddings have been tasked with an ever-increasing set of retrieval tasks over the years, with a nascent rise in using them for reasoning, instruction-following, coding, and more. These new benchmarks push embeddings to work for any query and any notion of relevance that could be given. While prior works have pointed out theoretical limitations of vector embeddings, there is a common assumption that these difficulties are exclusively due to unrealistic queries, and those that are not can be overcome with better training data and larger models. In this work, we demonstrate that we may encounter these theoretical limitations in realistic settings with extremely simple queries. We connect known results in learning theory, showing that the number of top-k subsets of documents capable of being returned as the result of some query is limited by the dimension of the embedding. We empirically show that this holds true even if we directly optimize on the test set with free parameterized embeddings. Using free embeddings, we then demonstrate that returning all pairs of documents requires a relatively high dimension. We then create a realistic dataset called LIMIT that stress tests embedding models based on these theoretical results, and observe that even state-of-the-art models fail on this dataset despite the simple nature of the task. Our work shows the limits of embedding models under the existing single vector paradigm and calls for future research to develop new techniques that can resolve this fundamental limitation.
Summary / 总结
Vector embeddings have been tasked with an ever-increasing set of retrieval tasks over the years, with a nascent rise in using them for reasoning, instruction-following, coding, and more.
本文研究了基于嵌入的检索系统的理论限制，挑战了认为这些限制仅源于不现实查询的普遍观点。通过连接学习理论，作者证明了可由向量嵌入检索的前k个文档子集数量受嵌入维度限制，无论训练数据或模型规模如何。实验证实，即使直接在测试集上优化自由嵌入，该限制依然存在，且返回所有文档对需要高维嵌入。作者构建了名为LIMIT的真实数据集，基于理论限制对嵌入模型进行压力测试，发现即使查询简单，当前最先进模型仍失败，揭示了单向量范式固有的局限性。
Increasing intelligence in AI agents can worsen collective outcomes
Authors: Neil F. Johnson
First: 2026-03-12T16:31:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:31:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When resources are scarce, will a population of AI agents coordinate in harmony, or descend into tribal chaos? Diverse decision-making AI from different developers is entering everyday devices -- from phones and medical devices to battlefield drones and cars -- and these AI agents typically compete for finite shared resources such as charging slots, relay bandwidth, and traffic priority. Yet their collective dynamics and hence risks to users and society are poorly understood. Here we study AI-agent populations as the first system of real agents in which four key variables governing collective behaviour can be independently toggled: nature (innate LLM diversity), nurture (individual reinforcement learning), culture (emergent tribe formation), and resource scarcity. We show empirically and mathematically that when resources are scarce, AI model diversity and reinforcement learning increase dangerous system overload, though tribe formation lessens this risk. Meanwhile, some individuals profit handsomely. When resources are abundant, the same ingredients drive overload to near zero, though tribe formation makes the overload slightly worse. The crossover is arithmetical: it is where opposing tribes that form spontaneously first fit inside the available capacity. More sophisticated AI-agent populations are not better: whether their sophistication helps or harms depends entirely on a single number -- the capacity-to-population ratio -- that is knowable before any AI-agent ships.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing deployment of diverse AI agents in resource-constrained environments, this study investigates how intelligence and behavior patterns affect collective outcomes. Using a controlled system where four variables—innate model diversity, individual reinforcement learning, emergent tribe formation, and resource scarcity—are independently adjustable, the researchers demonstrate that increased AI sophistication can paradoxically worsen system overload under scarcity, especially when combined with reinforcement learning. Tribe formation mitigates this risk, while individual agents may benefit disproportionately. Under abundant resources, the same factors lead to near-zero overload, though tribes slightly exacerbate it. The critical tipping point is determined by the capacity-to-population ratio, revealing that more intelligent agents do not inherently improve collective outcomes.
该研究探讨了在资源稀缺条件下AI群体行为的变化，动机源于日益普及的多样化AI系统在关键领域部署带来的潜在风险。研究人员通过调节四个核心变量——模型先天多样性、个体强化学习、部落自发形成和资源可用性——观察系统整体表现。实验发现，AI能力越强、多样性越高，在资源稀缺时越易引发系统过载，而部落形成可缓解此风险，尽管部分个体因此获益更大。资源充足时，过载几乎消失，但部落形成反而略微加剧过载。关键在于一个单一指标：容量与群体规模之比，该指标可在AI部署前预估其影响。
Hoi3DGen: Generating High-Quality Human-Object-Interactions in 3D
Authors: Agniv Sharma, Xianghui Xie, Tom Fischer, Eddy Ilg, Gerard Pons-Moll
First: 2026-03-12T16:27:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:27:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modeling and generating 3D human-object interactions from text is crucial for applications in AR, XR, and gaming. Existing approaches often rely on score distillation from text-to-image models, but their results suffer from the Janus problem and do not follow text prompts faithfully due to the scarcity of high-quality interaction data. We introduce Hoi3DGen, a framework that generates high-quality textured meshes of human-object interaction that follow the input interaction descriptions precisely. We first curate realistic and high-quality interaction data leveraging multimodal large language models, and then create a full text-to-3D pipeline, which achieves orders-of-magnitude improvements in interaction fidelity. Our method surpasses baselines by 4-15x in text consistency and 3-7x in 3D model quality, exhibiting strong generalization to diverse categories and interaction types, while maintaining high-quality 3D generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Hoi3DGen：生成高质量的人机交互3D模型
从文本建模和生成3D人机交互对对于AR、XR和游戏应用至关重要。现有方法通常依赖于从文本到图像模型的分数蒸馏，但其结果因高质量交互数据稀缺而面临雅努斯问题，无法忠实遵循文本提示。我们提出了Hoi3DGen框架，可精确生成符合输入交互描述的高质量纹理化3D人机交互模型。我们首先利用多模态大语言模型整理真实且高质量的交互数据，然后构建完整的文本到3D生成管道，显著提升了交互保真度。我们的方法在文本一致性上比基线提升4-15倍，在3D模型质量上提升3-7倍，展现出对多样类别和交互类型的强泛化能力，同时保持高质量3D生成。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for accurate 3D human-object interaction generation for AR, XR, and gaming, Hoi3DGen addresses the limitations of existing text-to-image-based methods that suffer from poor fidelity and prompt misalignment due to scarce high-quality data. The framework first constructs a curated dataset of realistic interactions using multimodal large language models, then builds a full text-to-3D pipeline that directly generates textured 3D meshes. Experimental results show Hoi3DGen outperforms baselines by 4-15x in text consistency and 3-7x in 3D model quality, with strong generalization across diverse interaction types and categories.
为满足AR、XR和游戏领域对高质量3D人机交互生成的需求，Hoi3DGen针对现有基于文本到图像方法因数据稀缺导致的保真度低和提示不匹配问题，提出全新解决方案。该框架首先利用多模态大语言模型构建真实高质量交互数据集，继而构建端到端文本到3D生成流水线，直接输出纹理化3D网格。实验表明，该方法在文本一致性上比基线高4-15倍，3D模型质量提升3-7倍，且在多种交互类型和类别上表现出强泛化能力。
WideSeek-R1: Exploring Width Scaling for Broad Information Seeking via Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Zelai Xu, Zhexuan Xu, Ruize Zhang, Chunyang Zhu, Shi Yu, Weilin Liu, Quanlu Zhang, Wenbo Ding, Chao Yu, Yu Wang
First: 2026-02-04T15:05:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:26:55+00:00
Comments: https://wideseek-r1.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent advancements in Large Language Models (LLMs) have largely focused on depth scaling, where a single agent solves long-horizon problems with multi-turn reasoning and tool use. However, as tasks grow broader, the key bottleneck shifts from individual competence to organizational capability. In this work, we explore a complementary dimension of width scaling with multi-agent systems to address broad information seeking. Existing multi-agent systems often rely on hand-crafted workflows and turn-taking interactions that fail to parallelize work effectively. To bridge this gap, we propose WideSeek-R1, a lead-agent-subagent framework trained via multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) to synergize scalable orchestration and parallel execution. By utilizing a shared LLM with isolated contexts and specialized tools, WideSeek-R1 jointly optimizes the lead agent and parallel subagents on a curated dataset of 20k broad information-seeking tasks. Extensive experiments show that WideSeek-R1-4B achieves an item F1 score of 40.0% on the WideSearch benchmark, which is comparable to the performance of single-agent DeepSeek-R1-671B. Furthermore, WideSeek-R1-4B exhibits consistent performance gains as the number of parallel subagents increases, highlighting the effectiveness of width scaling.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of depth scaling in large language models for broad information seeking, this work introduces WideSeek-R1, a multi-agent reinforcement learning framework that scales via width rather than depth. The system employs a lead-agent-subagent architecture with shared LLMs and isolated contexts, enabling parallel subagents to execute tasks concurrently while being jointly optimized via MARL. Evaluated on 20k broad information-seeking tasks, WideSeek-R1-4B achieves an F1 score of 40.0% on the WideSearch benchmark, matching the performance of a 671B single-agent model, and demonstrates consistent performance gains as the number of parallel subagents increases, validating the effectiveness of width scaling.
为应对大语言模型在广域信息检索任务中深度扩展的局限性，本文提出WideSeek-R1多智能体强化学习框架，通过宽度扩展而非深度扩展提升性能。系统采用主智能体-子智能体架构，共享LLM并隔离上下文，实现并行任务执行与协同调度。在2万条广域信息检索任务数据集上训练后，WideSeek-R1-4B在WideSearch基准测试中达到40.0%的F1分数，与671B参数的单智能体模型表现相当，并随并行子智能体数量增加持续提升性能，验证了宽度扩展的有效性。
Cross-Context Review: Improving LLM Output Quality by Separating Production and Review Sessions
Authors: Tae-Eun Song
First: 2026-03-12T16:26:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:26:38+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 2 figures, 8 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models struggle to catch errors in their own outputs when the review happens in the same session that produced them. This paper introduces Cross-Context Review (CCR), a straightforward method where the review is conducted in a fresh session with no access to the production conversation history. We ran a controlled experiment: 30 artifacts (code, technical documents, presentation scripts) with 150 injected errors, tested under four review conditions -- same-session Self-Review (SR), repeated Self-Review (SR2), context-aware Subagent Review (SA), and Cross-Context Review (CCR). Over 360 reviews, CCR reached an F1 of 28.6%, outperforming SR (24.6%, p=0.008, d=0.52), SR2 (21.7%, p<0.001, d=0.72), and SA (23.8%, p=0.004, d=0.57). The SR2 result matters most for interpretation: reviewing twice in the same session did not beat reviewing once (p=0.11), which rules out repetition as an explanation for CCR's advantage. The benefit comes from context separation itself. CCR works with any model, needs no infrastructure, and costs only one extra session.
中文标题/摘要
标题：跨上下文审查：通过分离生成与审查会话提升大语言模型输出质量
大语言模型在生成输出的同一会话中难以发现自身错误。本文提出跨上下文审查（CCR）方法，即在无生成会话历史的新会话中进行审查。我们进行了受控实验：30个（代码、技术文档、演示脚本）人工注入150个错误，测试四种审查方式——同会话自审查（SR）、重复自审查（SR2）、上下文感知子代理审查（SA）和跨上下文审查（CCR）。在360次审查中，CCR达到F1值28.6%，优于SR（24.6%，p=0.008，d=0.52）、SR2（21.7%，p<0.001，d=0.72）和SA（23.8%，p=0.004，d=0.57）。SR2结果对解释至关重要：同一会话内重复审查并未优于单次审查（p=0.11），排除重复作为CCR优势的原因。优势源于上下文分离本身。CCR适用于任何模型，无需基础设施，仅需额外一个会话。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the limitation of large language models in detecting errors when reviewing their own outputs within the same session. It proposes Cross-Context Review (CCR), a method that isolates review into a separate session with no access to prior production context. In a controlled experiment with 30 artifacts and 150 injected errors, CCR achieved an F1 score of 28.6%, significantly outperforming same-session self-review (24.6%), repeated self-review (21.7%), and subagent review (23.8%). The lack of improvement from repeated self-review suggests the benefit stems from context separation rather than repetition, and CCR requires no infrastructure or additional training, only one extra session.
该论文指出，大语言模型在生成内容时若在同一会话中进行自我审查，往往难以发现自身错误。作者提出跨上下文审查（CCR）方法，将生成与审查分置于不同会话，且审查会话无法访问生成过程的历史对话。在包含30个含150处注入错误的工件的对照实验中，CCR达到28.6%的F1分数，显著优于同会话自审（24.6%，p=0.008）、重复自审（21.7%，p<0.001）和子代理审查（23.8%，p=0.004）。关键发现是，重复自审未带来性能提升（p=0.11），说明CCR的优势源于上下文分离而非重复机制，且该方法无需特殊基础设施，仅需额外一个会话即可实现。
From Toil to Thought: Designing for Strategic Exploration and Responsible AI in Systematic Literature Reviews
Authors: Runlong Ye, Naaz Sibia, Angela Zavaleta Bernuy, Tingting Zhu, Carolina Nobre, Viktoria Pammer-Schindler, Michael Liut
First: 2026-01-21T00:28:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:25:08+00:00
Comments: Accepted at IUI 26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Systematic Literature Reviews (SLRs) are fundamental to scientific progress, yet the process is hindered by a fragmented tool ecosystem that imposes a high cognitive load. This friction suppresses the iterative, exploratory nature of scholarly work. To investigate these challenges, we conducted an exploratory design study with 20 experienced researchers. This study identified key friction points: 1) the high cognitive load of managing iterative query refinement across multiple databases, 2) the overwhelming scale and pace of publication of modern literature, and 3) the tension between automation and scholarly agency. Informed by these findings, we developed ARC, a design probe that operationalizes solutions for multi-database integration, transparent iterative search, and verifiable AI-assisted screening. A comparative user study with 8 researchers suggests that an integrated environment facilitates a transition in scholarly work, moving researchers from managing administrative overhead to engaging in strategic exploration. By utilizing external representations to scaffold strategic exploration and transparent AI reasoning, our system supports verifiable judgment, aiming to augment expert contributions from initial creation through long-term maintenance of knowledge synthesis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从劳作到思考：系统文献综述中的战略探索与负责任AI设计
系统文献综述（SLR）是科学进步的基础，但其过程受制于碎片化的工具生态系统，带来高认知负荷，抑制了学术工作的迭代探索特性。为探究这些挑战，我们对20名经验丰富的研究人员进行了探索性设计研究。研究识别出三大摩擦点：1）在多个数据库间管理迭代查询优化的高认知负荷；2）现代文献出版规模庞大且节奏迅猛；3）自动化与学术自主性之间的张力。基于这些发现，我们开发了ARC设计探针，实现多数据库集成、透明迭代搜索及可验证的AI辅助筛选。对8名研究人员的对比用户研究表明，集成环境有助于学术工作转型，使研究人员从管理行政负担转向战略探索。通过外部表征支持战略探索与透明AI推理，系统旨在增强专家从知识创建到长期维护的贡献。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the cognitive burden and fragmented tools that hinder systematic literature reviews, this study engaged 20 experienced researchers to identify key friction points, including multi-database query management, overwhelming publication volume, and tension between automation and scholarly agency. Based on these insights, the authors designed ARC, a design probe that integrates multi-database search, transparent iterative querying, and verifiable AI-assisted screening. A comparative user study with 8 researchers demonstrated that ARC reduces administrative overhead and enables researchers to shift toward strategic exploration, supported by external representations and transparent AI reasoning, thereby enhancing verifiable judgment throughout knowledge synthesis.
为解决系统性文献综述中工具碎片化导致的认知负担问题，本研究通过20名资深研究者识别出三大核心痛点：多数据库查询管理复杂、文献出版规模与速度过载、自动化与学者自主性之间的张力。基于此，研究团队设计了ARC原型，整合多数据库协同、透明迭代搜索与可验证AI筛选功能。对8名研究者的对比实验表明，ARC帮助研究者从处理行政性任务转向战略探索，通过外部表征与透明AI推理支持可验证判断，从而提升从知识创建到长期维护的综合能力。
CRAFT: A Tendon-Driven Hand with Hybrid Hard-Soft Compliance
Authors: Leo Lin, Shivansh Patel, Jay Moon, Svetlana Lazebnik, Unnat Jain
First: 2026-03-12T16:23:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:23:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We introduce CRAFT hand, a tendon-driven anthropomorphic hand with hybrid hard-soft compliance for contact-rich manipulation. The design is based on a simple idea: contact is not uniform across the hand. Impacts concentrate at joints, while links carry most of the load. CRAFT places soft material at joints and keeps links rigid, and uses rollingcontact joint surfaces to keep flexion on repeatable motion paths. Fifteen motors mounted on the fingers drive the hand through tendons, keeping the form factor compact and the fingers light. In structural tests, CRAFT improves strength and endurance while maintaining comparable repeatability. In teleoperation, CRAFT improves handling of fragile and low-friction items, and the hand covers 33/33 grasps in the Feix taxonomy. The full design costs under $600 and will be released open-source with visionbased teleoperation and simulation integration. Project page: http://craft-hand.github.io/
中文标题/摘要
标题：CRAFT：一种基于腱驱动与混合刚柔并济的仿人手
我们介绍了CRAFT手，一种用于接触密集操作的腱驱动仿人手，具备混合刚柔并济特性。其设计基于一个简单理念：手部接触并非均匀分布，冲击集中在关节处，而连杆承担大部分载荷。CRAFT在关节处布置柔性材料，保持连杆刚性，并采用滚动接触关节表面以确保屈曲运动路径可重复。十五个安装在手指上的电机通过肌腱驱动手部，使整体结构紧凑且手指轻便。结构测试表明，CRAFT在保持相近重复精度的同时提升了强度与耐久性。在遥操作中，CRAFT提升了对易碎及低摩擦物品的操控能力，并覆盖了Feix分类法中的33/33种抓取方式。完整设计成本低于600美元，将开源发布，包含基于视觉的遥操作与仿真集成。项目页面：http://craft-hand.github.io/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for hands that can handle delicate and high-friction manipulation tasks with robustness and repeatability, CRAFT introduces a tendon-driven anthropomorphic hand using hybrid hard-soft compliance, placing soft materials at joints to absorb impact while keeping links rigid for load-bearing. The hand employs rolling-contact joint surfaces to ensure repeatable motion and is actuated by 15 motors mounted on fingers, enabling compactness and lightweight design. Experimental results show improved structural strength and endurance without sacrificing repeatability, enhanced performance in teleoperation for fragile and low-friction objects, and full coverage of the Feix grasp taxonomy (33/33).
为实现高鲁棒性、高接触性操作同时兼顾灵巧性与成本，CRAFT提出一种腱驱动仿人手，采用硬软混合结构。关节处布置柔性材料以缓冲冲击，指节保持刚性以承载负载，并通过滚动接触表面确保运动重复性。15个安装于手指的电机通过肌腱驱动，实现紧凑轻量结构。实验表明，该设计在保持高重复性的同时显著提升结构强度与耐久性。在遥操作中，CRAFT对易碎及低摩擦物体处理更优，可完成Feix分类中的全部33种抓取动作，整机成本低于600美元。
RefTr: Recurrent Refinement of Confluent Trajectories for 3D Vascular Tree Centerlines
Authors: Roman Naeem, David Hagerman, Jennifer Alvén, Fredrik Kahl
First: 2025-11-25T20:22:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:22:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Tubular tree structures such as blood vessels and lung airways are central to many clinical tasks, including diagnosis, treatment planning, and surgical navigation. Accurate centerline extraction with correct topology is essential, as missing small branches can lead to incomplete assessments or overlooked abnormalities. We propose RefTr, a 3D image-to-graph framework that generates vascular centerlines via recurrent refinement of confluent trajectories. RefTr adopts a Transformer-based Producer-Refiner architecture in which the Producer predicts candidate trajectories and a shared Refiner iteratively refines them toward the target branches. The confluent trajectory representation enables whole-branch refinement while explicitly enforcing valid topology. This recurrent scheme improves precision and reduces decoder parameters by 2.4x compared to the state-of-the-art. We further introduce an efficient non-maximum suppression algorithm for spatial tree graphs to merge duplicate branches and extend evaluation metrics to be radius-aware for robust comparison. Experiments on multiple public datasets demonstrate stronger overall performance, faster inference, and substantially fewer parameters, highlighting the effectiveness of RefTr for 3D vascular tree analysis.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate and topologically correct centerline extraction in 3D vascular trees for clinical applications, RefTr introduces a Transformer-based Producer-Refiner architecture that recurrently refines confluent trajectories to generate precise centerlines. The Producer generates candidate trajectories, while a shared Refiner iteratively improves them toward target branches, enforcing valid topology through confluent representation. Experimental results on multiple public datasets show RefTr achieves superior performance, faster inference, and 2.4x fewer decoder parameters than state-of-the-art methods, while also introducing radius-aware evaluation metrics and an efficient branch merging algorithm.
为满足临床任务中对3D血管树中心线精确提取与拓扑正确性的需求，RefTr提出一种基于递归优化汇合轨迹的3D图像到图框架。该方法采用Transformer架构的生产者-精炼器结构：生产者生成候选轨迹，共享精炼器迭代优化至目标分支并显式保持拓扑有效性。在多个公开数据集上的实验表明，RefTr在整体性能、推理速度和参数量（减少2.4倍）上优于当前最优方法，同时引入半径感知评估指标和高效分支合并算法，进一步提升鲁棒性。
Cornserve: A Distributed Serving System for Any-to-Any Multimodal Models
Authors: Jae-Won Chung, Jeff J. Ma, Jisang Ahn, Yizhuo Liang, Akshay Jajoo, Myungjin Lee, Mosharaf Chowdhury
Venue: www
First: 2026-03-12T16:20:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:20:35+00:00
Comments: Open source https://github.com/cornserve-ai/cornserve / Demo video https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nb8R-vztLRg
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Any-to-Any models are an emerging class of multimodal models that accept combinations of multimodal data (e.g., text, image, video, audio) as input and generate them as output. Serving these models are challenging; different requests with different input and output modalities traverse different paths through the model computation graph, and each component of the model have different scaling characteristics. We present Cornserve, a distributed serving system for generic Any-to-Any models. Cornserve provides a flexible task abstraction for expressing Any-to-Any model computation graphs, enabling component disaggregation and independent scaling. The distributed runtime dispatches compute to the data plane via an efficient record-and-replay execution model that keeps track of data dependencies, and forwards tensor data between components directly from the producer to the consumer. Built on Kubernetes with approximately 23K new lines of Python, Cornserve supports diverse Any-to-Any models and delivers up to 3.81$\times$ higher throughput and 5.79$\times$ lower tail latency. Cornserve is open-source, and the demo video is available on YouTube.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Cornserve：面向任意模态间模型的分布式服务系统
任意模态间模型是一类新兴的多模态模型，可接受多种模态数据（如文本、图像、视频、音频）的组合作为输入，并生成相应模态的输出。服务此类模型具有挑战性：不同请求因输入与输出模态不同，需经过模型计算图中不同路径，且模型各组件具有不同的扩展特性。 我们提出Cornserve，一个通用任意模态间模型的分布式服务系统。Cornserve提供灵活的任务抽象，用于表达任意模态间模型的计算图，支持组件解耦与独立扩展。分布式运行时通过高效的记录-重放执行模型，跟踪数据依赖关系，并直接将张量数据从生产者转发至消费者。基于Kubernetes构建，新增约23K行Python代码，Cornserve支持多种任意模态间模型，实现最高3.81倍吞吐量提升和5.79倍尾延迟降低。Cornserve开源，演示视频可在YouTube观看。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the complexity of serving Any-to-Any multimodal models that handle diverse input-output modalities with varying scaling needs, Cornserve introduces a distributed serving system that abstracts model computation into flexible tasks, enabling component disaggregation and independent scaling. It employs a record-and-replay execution model to efficiently dispatch compute via the data plane, forwarding tensor data directly between components based on dependencies. Experiments show Cornserve achieves up to 3.81x higher throughput and 5.79x lower tail latency compared to baseline systems, while being built on Kubernetes with minimal code overhead.
为应对Any-to-Any多模态模型因输入输出模态多样、组件扩展性不一带来的服务挑战，Cornserve提出一种分布式服务系统，通过灵活的任务抽象建模计算图，支持组件解耦与独立扩展。其采用记录-重放执行模型高效调度计算，并直接在数据平面转发张量数据。实验表明，Cornserve相较基线系统吞吐量提升最高达3.81倍，尾延迟降低最高达5.79倍，且支持多种Any-to-Any模型在Kubernetes上运行。
On the Reliability of Cue Conflict and Beyond
Authors: Pum Jun Kim, Seung-Ah Lee, Seongho Park, Dongyoon Han, Jaejun Yoo
First: 2026-03-11T14:40:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:19:56+00:00
Comments: Shape-Texture Bias, Cue Conflict Benchmark
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Understanding how neural networks rely on visual cues offers a human-interpretable view of their internal decision processes. The cue-conflict benchmark has been influential in probing shape-texture preference and in motivating the insight that stronger, human-like shape bias is often associated with improved in-domain performance. However, we find that the current stylization-based instantiation can yield unstable and ambiguous bias estimates. Specifically, stylization may not reliably instantiate perceptually valid and separable cues nor control their relative informativeness, ratio-based bias can obscure absolute cue sensitivity, and restricting evaluation to preselected classes can distort model predictions by ignoring the full decision space. Together, these factors can confound preference with cue validity, cue balance, and recognizability artifacts. We introduce REFINED-BIAS, an integrated dataset and evaluation framework for reliable and interpretable shape-texture bias diagnosis. REFINED-BIAS constructs balanced, human- and model- recognizable cue pairs using explicit definitions of shape and texture, and measures cue-specific sensitivity over the full label space via a ranking-based metric, enabling fairer cross-model comparisons. Across diverse training regimes and architectures, REFINED-BIAS enables fairer cross-model comparison, more faithful diagnosis of shape and texture biases, and clearer empirical conclusions, resolving inconsistencies that prior cue-conflict evaluations could not reliably disambiguate.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关于线索冲突的可靠性及其超越
理解神经网络如何依赖视觉线索，可提供人类可解释的内部决策过程视角。线索冲突基准在探究形状-纹理偏好方面具有重要影响，并促使人们认识到更强的人类类形状偏见通常与更好的领域内性能相关。然而，我们发现当前基于风格化的方法可能产生不稳定且模糊的偏见估计。具体而言，风格化可能无法可靠地实例化感知上有效且可分离的线索，也无法控制其相对信息量；基于比率的偏见可能掩盖绝对线索敏感性；将评估限制在预选类别内可能通过忽略完整决策空间而扭曲模型预测。这些因素共同可能导致偏好与线索有效性、线索平衡及可识别性伪影混淆。我们提出了REFINED-BIAS，一个用于可靠且可解释的形状-纹理偏见诊断的集成数据集与评估框架。REFINED-BIAS通过明确的形状与纹理定义构建平衡且人类与模型均可识别的线索对，并通过基于排序的指标在完整标签空间内测量线索特异性敏感性，从而实现更公平的跨模型比较。
Summary / 总结
Understanding how neural networks rely on visual cues offers a human-interpretable view of their internal decision processes.
该论文指出当前基于风格化图像的线索冲突基准在测量神经网络的形状-纹理偏置时存在不稳定和模糊的问题，原因包括风格化导致的感知线索不可靠、比值型偏置指标掩盖绝对敏感性、以及仅评估预选类别导致决策空间失真。作者提出REFINED-BIAS框架，通过明确的形状与纹理定义构建平衡且人与模型均可识别的线索对，并采用基于排序的指标在完整标签空间中测量各线索的敏感性。实验表明，该框架能实现更公平、准确和可解释的跨模型比较，有效解决以往基准的混淆问题，为形状与纹理偏置提供更清晰的实证结论。
SommBench: Assessing Sommelier Expertise of Language Models
Authors: William Brach, Tomas Bedej, Jacob Nielsen, Jacob Pichna, Juraj Bedej, Eemeli Saarensilta, Julie Dupouy, Gianluca Barmina, Andrea Blasi Núñez, Peter Schneider-Kamp, Kristian Košťál, Michal Ries, Lukas Galke Poech
First: 2026-03-12T16:19:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:19:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
With the rapid advances of large language models, it becomes increasingly important to systematically evaluate their multilingual and multicultural capabilities. Previous cultural evaluation benchmarks focus mainly on basic cultural knowledge that can be encoded in linguistic form. Here, we propose SommBench, a multilingual benchmark to assess sommelier expertise, a domain deeply grounded in the senses of smell and taste. While language models learn about sensory properties exclusively through textual descriptions, SommBench tests whether this textual grounding is sufficient to emulate expert-level sensory judgment. SommBench comprises three main tasks: Wine Theory Question Answering (WTQA), Wine Feature Completion (WFC), and Food-Wine Pairing (FWP). SommBench is available in multiple languages: English, Slovak, Swedish, Finnish, German, Danish, Italian, and Spanish. This helps separate a language model's wine expertise from its language skills. The benchmark datasets were developed in close collaboration with a professional sommelier and native speakers of the respective languages, resulting in 1,024 wine theory question-answering questions, 1,000 wine feature-completion examples, and 1,000 food-wine pairing examples. We provide results for the most popular language models, including closed-weights models such as Gemini 2.5, and open-weights models, such as GPT-OSS and Qwen 3. Our results show that the most capable models perform well on wine theory question answering (up to 97% correct with a closed-weights model), yet feature completion (peaking at 65%) and food-wine pairing show (MCC ranging between 0 and 0.39) turn out to be more challenging. These results position SommBench as an interesting and challenging benchmark for evaluating the sommelier expertise of language models. The benchmark is publicly available at https://github.com/sommify/sommbench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SommBench：评估语言模型的品酒师专业能力
随着大语言模型的快速发展，系统评估其多语言和跨文化能力变得愈发重要。以往的文化评估基准主要关注可编码为语言形式的基本文化知识。本文提出SommBench，一个用于评估品酒师专业能力的多语言基准，该领域深深植根于嗅觉与味觉感官。尽管语言模型仅通过文本描述学习感官属性，SommBench旨在检验这种文本基础是否足以模拟专家级感官判断。SommBench包含三个主要任务：葡萄酒理论问答（WTQA）、葡萄酒特征补全（WFC）和食物-葡萄酒搭配（FWP）。该基准提供多种语言版本：英语、斯洛伐克语、瑞典语、芬兰语、德语、丹麦语、意大利语和西班牙语，有助于将语言模型的葡萄酒专业知识与其语言能力区分开来。基准数据集在专业品酒师与各语言母语者紧密合作下开发，包含1024个葡萄酒理论问答题、1000个葡萄酒特征补全示例和1000个食物-葡萄酒搭配示例。我们提供了主流语言模型的结果，包括闭源模型如Gemini 2.5，以及开源模型如GPT-OSS和Qwen 3。结果显示，最强大的模型在葡萄酒理论问答中表现优异（闭源模型准确率高达97%），但在特征补全（最高65%）和食物-葡萄酒搭配（MCC介于0至0.39之间）任务中更具挑战性。这些结果使SommBench成为评估语言模型品酒师专业能力的一个有趣且具有挑战性的基准。该基准公开获取地址为https://github.com/sommify/sommbench。

Prompting Underestimates LLM Capability for Time Series Classification
Authors: Dan Schumacher, Erfan Nourbakhsh, Rocky Slavin, Anthony Rios
First: 2026-01-06T23:31:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:17:41+00:00
Comments: 8 pages + Appendix and References, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Prompt-based evaluations suggest that large language models (LLMs) perform poorly on time series classification, raising doubts about whether they encode meaningful temporal structure. We show that this conclusion reflects limitations of prompt-based generation rather than the model's representational capacity by directly comparing prompt outputs with linear probes over the same internal representations. While zero-shot prompting performs near chance, linear probes improve average F1 from 0.15-0.26 to 0.61-0.67, often matching or exceeding specialized time series models. Layer-wise analyses further show that class-discriminative time series information emerges in early transformer layers and is amplified by visual and multimodal inputs. Together, these results demonstrate a systematic mismatch between what LLMs internally represent and what prompt-based evaluation reveals, leading current evaluations to underestimate their time series understanding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：提示法低估了大语言模型在时间序列分类中的能力
基于提示的评估表明，大语言模型（LLMs）在时间序列分类任务中表现不佳，引发对其是否编码有意义的时间结构的质疑。我们通过直接比较提示输出与线性探针在相同内部表示上的表现，表明这一结论反映的是提示生成的局限性，而非模型的表征能力。虽然零样本提示表现接近随机水平，但线性探针将平均F1分数从0.15-0.26提升至0.61-0.67，常与或超越专用时间序列模型相当。分层分析进一步表明，具有类区分能力的时间序列信息在Transformer早期层中出现，并被视觉和多模态输入放大。这些结果共同表明，LLMs内部表征与提示评估揭示的内容存在系统性不匹配，导致当前评估低估了其对时间序列的理解能力。

Taming the Adversary: Stable Minimax Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient via Fractional Objectives
Authors: Taeho Lee, Donghwan Lee
First: 2026-03-12T16:15:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:15:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning (RL) has achieved remarkable success in a wide range of control and decision-making tasks. However, RL agents often exhibit unstable or degraded performance when deployed in environments subject to unexpected external disturbances and model uncertainties. Consequently, ensuring reliable performance under such conditions remains a critical challenge. In this paper, we propose minimax deep deterministic policy gradient (MMDDPG), a framework for learning disturbance-resilient policies in continuous control tasks. The training process is formulated as a minimax optimization problem between a user policy and an adversarial disturbance policy. In this problem, the user learns a robust policy that minimizes the objective function, while the adversary generates disturbances that maximize it. To stabilize this interaction, we introduce a fractional objective that balances task performance and disturbance magnitude. This objective prevents excessively aggressive disturbances and promotes robust learning. Experimental evaluations in MuJoCo environments demonstrate that the proposed MMDDPG achieves significantly improved robustness against both external force perturbations and model parameter variations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：驯服对手：通过分数目标实现稳定的最小最大深度确定性策略梯度
强化学习（RL）在广泛的控制与决策任务中取得了显著成功。然而，RL代理在面临意外外部干扰和模型不确定性时，往往表现出不稳定或性能下降。因此，在此类条件下确保可靠性能仍是一个关键挑战。本文提出最小最大深度确定性策略梯度（MMDDPG），一种用于连续控制任务中学习抗干扰策略的框架。训练过程被建模为用户策略与对抗性干扰策略之间的最小最大优化问题。在此问题中，用户学习一个鲁棒策略以最小化目标函数，而对手生成最大化该函数的干扰。为稳定这种交互，我们引入一个分数目标，平衡任务性能与干扰幅度。该目标可防止过度激进的干扰，并促进鲁棒学习。在MuJoCo环境中的实验表明，所提出的MMDDPG在对抗外部力扰动和模型参数变化方面显著提升了鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability of reinforcement learning agents under external disturbances and model uncertainties, this paper introduces MMDDPG, a minimax framework that trains a user policy to be robust against adversarial disturbances. The method formulates learning as a minimax optimization where the user minimizes a fractional objective balancing task performance and disturbance magnitude, while an adversary maximizes the same objective. Experiments in MuJoCo environments show that MMDDPG significantly outperforms baseline methods in resisting external force perturbations and model parameter variations, achieving more stable and reliable policy performance.
为解决强化学习代理在外部干扰和模型不确定性下表现不稳定的问题，本文提出MMDDPG框架，通过用户策略与对抗性干扰策略的最小最大优化训练鲁棒策略。该方法引入分数目标函数，在任务性能与干扰强度间取得平衡，避免干扰过于激进。在MuJoCo环境中的实验表明，MMDDPG在外部力扰动和模型参数变化下显著优于基线方法。
On Information Self-Locking in Reinforcement Learning for Active Reasoning of LLM agents
Authors: Deyu Zou, Yongqiang Chen, Fan Feng, Mufei Li, Pan Li, Yu Gong, James Cheng
First: 2026-03-12T16:14:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:14:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning (RL) with outcome-based rewards has achieved significant success in training large language model (LLM) agents for complex reasoning tasks. However, in active reasoning where agents need to strategically ask questions to acquire task-relevant information, we find that LLM agents trained with RL often suffer from information self-locking: the agent ceases to ask informative questions and struggles to internalize already-obtained information. To understand the phenomenon, we decompose active reasoning into two core capabilities: Action Selection (AS), which determines the observation stream through queries, and Belief Tracking (BT), which updates the agent's belief based on collected evidence. We show that deficient AS and BT capabilities will limit the information exploration during RL training. Furthermore, insufficient exploration in turn hinders the improvement of AS and BT, creating a feedback loop that locks the agent in a low-information regime. To resolve the issue, we propose a simple yet effective approach that reallocates the learning signal by injecting easy- to-obtain directional critiques to help the agent escape self-locking. Extensive experiments with 7 datasets show that our approach significantly mitigates the information self-locking, bringing up to 60% improvements.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关于强化学习中LLM代理主动推理的信息自锁现象
基于结果奖励的强化学习（RL）在训练大型语言模型（LLM）代理完成复杂推理任务方面取得了显著成功。然而，在需要代理战略性提问以获取任务相关信息的主动推理场景中，我们发现通过RL训练的LLM代理常出现信息自锁现象：代理停止提出有信息量的问题，并难以内化已获得的信息。为理解该现象，我们将主动推理分解为两个核心能力：动作选择（AS），通过提问决定观察流；信念追踪（BT），根据收集到的证据更新代理信念。我们表明，AS和BT能力不足会限制RL训练期间的信息探索。此外，探索不足反过来又阻碍AS和BT能力的提升，形成一个反馈循环，使代理陷入低信息状态。为解决此问题，我们提出一种简单而有效的方法，通过注入易于获取的方向性批评，重新分配学习信号，帮助代理摆脱自锁。在7个数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法显著缓解了信息自锁，带来最高达60%的性能提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that LLM agents trained via reinforcement learning often fail to ask informative questions in active reasoning tasks, leading to information self-locking, this work identifies two core components—Action Selection and Belief Tracking—that jointly constrain information exploration. The authors demonstrate that insufficient AS and BT capabilities create a feedback loop where poor exploration hinders further improvement of these components, trapping agents in low-information states. To break this cycle, they introduce a method that injects easy-to-obtain directional critiques to reallocate learning signals, enabling agents to escape self-locking. Experiments across seven datasets show this approach significantly improves performance, achieving up to 60% gains.
为了解决大语言模型在主动推理中因强化学习导致的信息自锁问题——即模型停止提问并难以内化已有信息——该研究将主动推理分解为动作选择与信念追踪两个核心能力，发现二者能力不足会形成负反馈循环，使模型陷入低信息状态。为此，作者提出注入易获取的定向批评以重新分配学习信号，帮助模型摆脱自锁。在七个数据集上的实验表明，该方法显著缓解了信息自锁，性能提升最高达60%。
EvoTok: A Unified Image Tokenizer via Residual Latent Evolution for Visual Understanding and Generation
Authors: Yan Li, Ning Liao, Xiangyu Zhao, Shaofeng Zhang, Xiaoxing Wang, Yifan Yang, Junchi Yan, Xue Yang
First: 2026-03-12T16:13:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:13:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The development of unified multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is fundamentally challenged by the granularity gap between visual understanding and generation: understanding requires high-level semantic abstractions, while image generation demands fine-grained pixel-level representations. Existing approaches usually enforce the two supervision on the same set of representation or decouple these two supervision on separate feature spaces, leading to interference and inconsistency, respectively. In this work, we propose EvoTok, a unified image tokenizer that reconciles these requirements through a residual evolution process within a shared latent space. Instead of maintaining separate token spaces for pixels and semantics, EvoTok encodes an image into a cascaded sequence of residual tokens via residual vector quantization. This residual sequence forms an evolution trajectory where earlier stages capture low-level details and deeper stages progressively transition toward high-level semantic representations. Despite being trained on a relatively modest dataset of 13M images, far smaller than the billion-scale datasets used by many previous unified tokenizers, EvoTok achieves a strong reconstruction quality of 0.43 rFID on ImageNet-1K at 256x256 resolution. When integrated with a large language model, EvoTok shows promising performance across 7 out of 9 visual understanding benchmarks, and remarkable results on image generation benchmarks such as GenEval and GenAI-Bench. These results demonstrate that modeling visual representations as an evolving trajectory provides an effective and principled solution for unifying visual understanding and generation.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of aligning visual understanding and generation through a unified representation, EvoTok introduces a residual latent evolution framework that encodes images into a cascaded sequence of residual tokens via residual vector quantization, enabling a shared latent space that evolves from low-level pixel details to high-level semantics. Trained on a modest 13M-image dataset, it achieves a strong reconstruction quality of 0.43 rFID on ImageNet-1K at 256x256 resolution and demonstrates strong performance across 7 out of 9 visual understanding benchmarks and notable results on image generation benchmarks like GenEval and GenAI-Bench, validating its effectiveness in unifying multimodal tasks.
为解决视觉理解与生成任务在表征粒度上的矛盾，EvoTok提出残差潜在演化框架，通过残差向量量化将图像编码为从低级像素细节逐步演化至高级语义的残差令牌序列，实现共享潜在空间下的统一表征。尽管仅在1300万张图像的小数据集上训练，其在ImageNet-1K 256x256分辨率下重建质量达0.43 rFID，并在9个视觉理解基准中的7个及图像生成基准GenEval和GenAI-Bench上表现优异，证明其在统一视觉理解与生成任务中的有效性。
To Words and Beyond: Probing Large Language Models for Sentence-Level Psycholinguistic Norms of Memorability and Reading Times
Authors: Thomas Hikaru Clark, Carlos Arriaga, Javier Conde, Gonzalo Martínez, Pedro Reviriego
First: 2026-03-12T16:10:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:10:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently been shown to produce estimates of psycholinguistic norms, such as valence, arousal, or concreteness, for words and multiword expressions, that correlate with human judgments. These estimates are obtained by prompting an LLM, in zero-shot fashion, with a question similar to those used in human studies. Meanwhile, for other norms such as lexical decision time or age of acquisition, LLMs require supervised fine-tuning to obtain results that align with ground-truth values. In this paper, we extend this approach to the previously unstudied features of sentence memorability and reading times, which involve the relationship between multiple words in a sentence-level context. Our results show that via fine-tuning, models can provide estimates that correlate with human-derived norms and exceed the predictive power of interpretable baseline predictors, demonstrating that LLMs contain useful information about sentence-level features. At the same time, our results show very mixed zero-shot and few-shot performance, providing further evidence that care is needed when using LLM-prompting as a proxy for human cognitive measures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从词到句：探索大型语言模型在句子层面记忆性与阅读时间心理语言学规范中的应用
近期研究表明，大型语言模型（LLMs）可零样本生成词及多词表达的心理语言学规范估计（如效价、唤醒度或具体性），其结果与人类判断相关。此类估计通过向LLM提出类似人类研究中使用的提问获得。而对于词汇决策时间或习得年龄等其他规范，LLMs需监督微调才能获得与真实值一致的结果。本文将此方法扩展至此前未被研究的句子层面特征——记忆性与阅读时间，这些特征涉及句子语境中多个词之间的关系。结果表明，经微调后模型可提供与人类规范相关且超越可解释基线预测器的预测能力，证明LLMs包含有关句子层面特征的有用信息。同时，我们的结果也显示零样本与少样本表现参差不齐，进一步表明在将LLM提示作为人类认知测量代理时需谨慎。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential of LLMs to estimate psycholinguistic norms, this study investigates whether LLMs can predict sentence-level memorability and reading times—features previously unexplored in this context. The method involves fine-tuning LLMs on human-derived norms for these features and comparing their performance against interpretable baselines. Experimental results show that fine-tuned models achieve strong correlations with human judgments and outperform baseline predictors, indicating that LLMs encode useful sentence-level linguistic knowledge. However, zero-shot and few-shot prompting yield inconsistent results, suggesting that LLMs cannot reliably substitute for human cognitive measures without supervision.
为探索大语言模型在句法层面心理语言学规范中的潜力，本研究将其应用于此前未被研究的句子记忆性与阅读时长特征，这些特征依赖于句内词语间的交互关系。通过微调，模型生成的估计值与人类数据高度相关，且优于可解释基线模型。然而，零样本和少样本提示表现不稳定，表明在用LLM提示代理人类认知测量时需谨慎。
Whleaper: A 10-DOF Flexible Bipedal Wheeled Robot
Authors: Yinglei Zhu, Sixiao He, Yan Ning, Zhenghao Qi, Zhuoyuan Yong, Yihua Qin, Jianyu Chen
Venue: IROS
First: 2025-04-30T16:07:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:09:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Wheel-legged robots combine the advantages of both wheeled robots and legged robots, offering versatile locomotion capabilities with excellent stability on challenging terrains and high efficiency on flat surfaces. However, existing wheel-legged robots typically have limited hip joint mobility compared to humans, while hip joint plays a crucial role in locomotion. In this paper, we introduce Whleaper, a novel 10-degree-of-freedom (DOF) bipedal wheeled robot, with 3 DOFs at the hip of each leg. Its humanoid joint design enables adaptable motion in complex scenarios, ensuring stability and flexibility. This paper introduces the details of Whleaper, with a focus on innovative mechanical design, control algorithms and system implementation. Firstly, stability stems from the increased DOFs at the hip, which expand the range of possible postures and improve the robot's foot-ground contact. Secondly, the extra DOFs also augment its mobility. During walking or sliding, more complex movements can be adopted to execute obstacle avoidance tasks. Thirdly, we utilize two control algorithms to implement multimodal motion for walking and sliding. By controlling specific DOFs of the robot, we conducted a series of simulations and practical experiments, demonstrating that a high-DOF hip joint design can effectively enhance the stability and flexibility of wheel-legged robots. Whleaper shows its capability to perform actions such as squatting, obstacle avoidance sliding, and rapid turning in real-world scenarios.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance stability and mobility on varied terrains, this paper presents Whleaper, a 10-DOF bipedal wheeled robot featuring 3 DOFs at each hip joint to surpass the limited hip mobility of existing wheel-legged robots. The design enables greater posture adaptability and improved foot-ground contact for stability, while also supporting complex motions like obstacle avoidance and rapid turning. Experimental results demonstrate that the high-DOF hip configuration significantly enhances locomotion flexibility and robustness, validating the effectiveness of the mechanical design and dual control algorithms for multimodal motion.
为提升轮腿机器人在复杂地形上的稳定性和机动性，本文提出Whleaper，一款具备10自由度的双足轮式机器人，每条腿髋部拥有3个自由度，突破现有轮腿机器人髋关节活动范围有限的局限。该设计增强了姿态适应性与足底接地稳定性，同时支持复杂动作如避障滑行和快速转向。实验结果表明，高自由度髋关节结构显著提升了机器人运动的灵活性与鲁棒性，验证了其机械结构与多模态控制算法的有效性。
FedSKD: Aggregation-free Model-heterogeneous Federated Learning via Multi-dimensional Similarity Knowledge Distillation for Medical Image Classification
Authors: Ziqiao Weng, Weidong Cai, Bo Zhou
First: 2025-03-23T05:33:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:08:46+00:00
Comments: Accepted at IEEE-TNNLS, 17 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Federated learning (FL) enables privacy-preserving collaborative model training without direct data sharing. Model-heterogeneous FL (MHFL) extends this paradigm by allowing clients to train personalized models with heterogeneous architectures tailored to their computational resources and application-specific needs. However, existing MHFL methods predominantly rely on centralized aggregation, which introduces scalability and efficiency bottlenecks, or impose restrictions requiring partially identical model architectures across clients. While peer-to-peer (P2P) FL removes server dependence, it suffers from model drift and knowledge dilution, limiting its effectiveness in heterogeneous settings. To address these challenges, we propose FedSKD, a novel MHFL framework that facilitates direct knowledge exchange through round-robin model circulation, eliminating the need for centralized aggregation while allowing fully heterogeneous model architectures across clients. FedSKD's key innovation lies in multi-dimensional similarity knowledge distillation, which enables bidirectional cross-client knowledge transfer at batch, pixel/voxel, and region levels for heterogeneous models in FL. This approach mitigates catastrophic forgetting and model drift through progressive reinforcement and distribution alignment while preserving model heterogeneity. Extensive evaluations on fMRI-based autism spectrum disorder diagnosis and skin lesion classification demonstrate that FedSKD outperforms state-of-the-art heterogeneous and homogeneous FL baselines, achieving superior personalization (client-specific accuracy) and generalization (cross-institutional adaptability). These findings underscore FedSKD's potential as a scalable and robust solution for real-world medical federated learning applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FedSKD：通过多维相似性知识蒸馏实现无聚合的模型异构联邦学习用于医学图像分类
联邦学习（FL）能够在不直接共享数据的前提下实现隐私保护的协作模型训练。模型异构联邦学习（MHFL）扩展了这一范式，允许客户端根据其计算资源和应用需求训练个性化模型，采用异构架构。然而，现有MHFL方法主要依赖集中式聚合，这引入了可扩展性和效率瓶颈，或要求客户端模型架构部分相同。虽然点对点（P2P）FL消除了服务器依赖，但其易受模型漂移和知识稀释影响，限制了其在异构环境中的有效性。为解决这些问题，我们提出FedSKD，一种新颖的MHFL框架，通过轮询模型循环实现直接知识交换，无需集中式聚合，同时允许客户端采用完全异构的模型架构。FedSKD的核心创新在于多维相似性知识蒸馏，它在批次、像素/体素和区域层面实现异构模型间的双向跨客户端知识迁移，通过渐进强化和分布对齐缓解灾难性遗忘和模型漂移，同时保留模型异构性。在基于fMRI的自闭症谱系障碍诊断和皮肤病变分类上的广泛评估表明，FedSKD优于当前最先进的异构和同构FL基线方法，在个性化（客户端特定准确率）和泛化能力（跨机构适应性）方面表现更优。这些结果凸显了FedSKD作为可扩展且稳健的现实世界医学联邦学习解决方案的潜力。

Motivated by the need for scalable, privacy-preserving federated learning in medical imaging with heterogeneous client models, FedSKD introduces an aggregation-free framework that enables direct knowledge exchange via round-robin model circulation. Its core method, multi-dimensional similarity knowledge distillation, facilitates bidirectional knowledge transfer across clients at batch, pixel/voxel, and region levels, aligning model distributions while preserving architectural heterogeneity. Experimental results on fMRI autism diagnosis and skin lesion classification show FedSKD outperforms existing FL baselines in both client-specific accuracy and cross-institutional generalization, demonstrating its effectiveness in real-world medical settings.
为解决医疗图像分类中异构客户端模型的联邦学习问题，FedSKD提出一种无需聚合的框架，通过轮询模型循环实现直接知识交换。其核心方法是多维相似性知识蒸馏，在批次、像素/体素和区域层面实现双向跨客户端知识传递，对齐模型分布同时保留架构异构性。在fMRI自闭症诊断和皮肤病变分类实验中，FedSKD在客户端特异性准确率和跨机构泛化能力上均优于现有联邦学习基线，验证了其在真实医疗场景中的有效性。
Wasserstein Gradient Flows for Batch Bayesian Optimal Experimental Design
Authors: Louis Sharrock
First: 2026-03-12T16:08:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:08:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bayesian optimal experimental design (BOED) provides a powerful, decision-theoretic framework for selecting experiments so as to maximise the expected utility of the data to be collected. In practice, however, its applicability can be limited by the difficulty of optimising the chosen utility. The expected information gain (EIG), for example, is often high-dimensional and strongly non-convex. This challenge is particularly acute in the batch setting, where multiple experiments are to be designed simultaneously. In this paper, we introduce a new approach to batch EIG-based BOED via a probabilistic lifting of the original optimisation problem to the space of probability measures. In particular, we propose to optimise an entropic regularisation of the expected utility over the space of design measures. Under mild conditions, we show that this objective admits a unique minimiser, which can be explicitly characterised in the form of a Gibbs distribution. The resulting design law can be used directly as a randomised batch-design policy, or as a computational relaxation from which a deterministic batch is extracted. To obtain scalable approximations when the batch size is large, we then consider two tractable restrictions of the full batch distribution: a mean-field family, and an i.i.d. product family. For the i.i.d. objective, and formally for its mean-field extension, we derive the corresponding Wasserstein gradient flow, characterise its long-time behaviour, and obtain particle-based algorithms via space-time discretisations. We also introduce doubly stochastic variants that combine interacting particle updates with Monte Carlo estimators of the EIG gradient. Finally, we illustrate the performance of the proposed methods in several numerical experiments, demonstrating their ability to explore multimodal optimisation landscapes and obtain high-utility batches in challenging examples.

标题：基于Wasserstein梯度流的批量贝叶斯最优实验设计
贝叶斯最优实验设计（BOED）提供了一个强大的决策理论框架，用于选择实验以最大化所收集数据的预期效用。然而，在实践中，由于所选效用的优化难度，其适用性可能受限。例如，预期信息增益（EIG）往往是高维且强非凸的。这一挑战在批量设置中尤为突出，其中需同时设计多个实验。本文提出了一种新的批量EIG基BOED方法，通过将原始优化问题概率提升至概率测度空间。具体而言，我们提出在设计测度空间上优化预期效用的熵正则化形式。在温和条件下，我们证明该目标函数存在唯一最小值，可显式表征为Gibbs分布。所得设计策略可直接作为随机批量设计策略，或作为计算松弛方案，从中提取确定性批量。为在批量规模较大时获得可扩展近似，我们考虑了完整批量分布的两个可处理限制：均场族和独立同分布（i.i.d.）乘积族。对于i.i.d.目标及其均场扩展，我们推导了相应的Wasserstein梯度流，刻画其长期行为，并通过时空离散化获得粒子算法。此外，我们引入双重随机变体，结合粒子交互更新与EIG梯度的蒙特卡洛估计。最后，我们在多个数值实验中展示了所提方法的性能，证明其在复杂示例中能够探索多模态优化景观并获得高效用批量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational difficulty of optimizing expected information gain in batch Bayesian optimal experimental design, this paper proposes a probabilistic reformulation that lifts the design problem into the space of probability measures, enabling optimization via entropic regularization. The resulting objective admits a unique Gibbs-form minimizer, which can be used as a randomized policy or relaxed to yield deterministic batches. To scale to large batch sizes, the authors consider mean-field and i.i.d. approximations, deriving Wasserstein gradient flows for both and proposing particle-based algorithms with doubly stochastic updates. Experiments demonstrate that these methods effectively navigate multimodal landscapes and generate high-utility experimental batches in complex scenarios.
为解决批量贝叶斯最优实验设计中期望信息增益优化的计算困难，本文提出将原问题概率化提升至测度空间，通过熵正则化进行优化，其解具有唯一性且可显式表示为吉布斯分布，可用作随机批量设计策略或确定性设计的松弛。为应对大规模批量场景，作者引入均值场与独立同分布近似，推导其对应的Wasserstein梯度流并设计粒子算法，结合蒙特卡洛梯度估计实现双随机更新。实验表明，所提方法能有效探索多峰优化空间，生成高效益实验批次。
Towards Dynamic Model Identification and Gravity Compensation for the dVRK-Si Patient Side Manipulator
Authors: Haoying Zhou, Hao Yang, Brendan Burkhart, Anton Deguet, Loris Fichera, Gregory S. Fischer, Jie Ying Wu, Peter Kazanzides
First: 2026-03-12T16:06:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:06:16+00:00
Comments: Submitted to IEEE Transactions on Medical Robotics and Bionics (T-MRB), under review. Open-source GitHub Repo: https://github.com/jhu-dvrk/dvrk_psm_dynamics_identification
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK) is widely used for research in robot-assisted surgery, but most modeling and control methods target the first-generation dVRK Classic. The recently introduced dVRK-Si, built from da Vinci Si hardware, features a redesigned Patient Side Manipulator (PSM) with substantially larger gravity loading, which can degrade control if unmodeled. This paper presents the first complete kinematic and dynamic modeling framework for the dVRK-Si PSM. We derive a modified DH kinematic model that captures the closed-chain parallelogram mechanism, formulate dynamics via the Euler-Lagrange method, and express inverse dynamics in a linear-in-parameters regressor form. Dynamic parameters are identified from data collected on a periodic excitation trajectory optimized for numerical conditioning and estimated by convex optimization with physical feasibility constraints. Using the identified model, we implement real-time gravity compensation and computed-torque feedforward in the dVRK control stack. Experiments on a physical dVRK-Si show that the gravity compensation reduces steady-state joint errors by 68-84% and decreases end-effector tip drift during static holds from 4.2 mm to 0.7 mm. Computed-torque feedforward further improves transient and position tracking accuracy. For sinusoidal trajectory tracking, computed-torque feedforward reduces position errors by 35% versus gravity-only feedforward and by 40% versus PID-only. The proposed pipeline supports reliable control, high-fidelity simulation, and learning-based automation on the dVRK-Si.

A Robust and Efficient Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Framework for Traffic Signal Control
Authors: Sheng-You Huang, Hsiao-Chuan Chang, Yen-Chi Chen, Ting-Han Wei, I-Hau Yeh, Sheng-Yao Kuan, Chien-Yao Wang, Hsuan-Han Lee, I-Chen Wu
First: 2026-03-12T16:02:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:02:28+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 4 tables, 8 figures. Under review in the 31st ITS World Congress 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement Learning (RL) in Traffic Signal Control (TSC) faces significant hurdles in real-world deployment due to limited generalization to dynamic traffic flow variations. Existing approaches often overfit static patterns and use action spaces incompatible with driver expectations. This paper proposes a robust Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) framework validated in the Vissim traffic simulator. The framework integrates three mechanisms: (1) Turning Ratio Randomization, a training strategy that exposes agents to dynamic turning probabilities to enhance robustness against unseen scenarios; (2) a stability-oriented Exponential Phase Duration Adjustment action space, which balances responsiveness and precision through cyclical, exponential phase adjustments; and (3) a Neighbor-Based Observation scheme utilizing the MAPPO algorithm with Centralized Training with Decentralized Execution (CTDE). By leveraging centralized updates, this approach approximates the efficacy of global observations while maintaining scalable local communication. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework outperforms standard RL baselines, reducing average waiting time by over 10%. The proposed model exhibits superior generalization in unseen traffic scenarios and maintains high control stability, offering a practical solution for adaptive signal control.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向交通信号控制的鲁棒高效多智能体强化学习框架
强化学习（RL）在交通信号控制（TSC）中的实际部署面临显著挑战，主要因难以适应动态交通流变化。现有方法常过度拟合静态模式，且动作空间与驾驶员预期不兼容。本文提出一种经Vissim交通仿真器验证的鲁棒多智能体强化学习（MARL）框架，集成三项机制：（1）转向比例随机化训练策略，通过动态转向概率增强对未见场景的鲁棒性；（2）以稳定性为导向的指数相位持续时间调整动作空间，通过循环指数相位调整平衡响应性与精度；（3）基于邻域观测方案，采用MAPPO算法结合集中训练与分散执行（CTDE）。该方法通过集中更新近似全局观测效果，同时保持可扩展的局部通信。实验表明，本框架优于标准RL基线，平均等待时间降低超10%，在未见交通场景中泛化能力更强，控制稳定性高，为自适应信号控制提供实用方案。

This paper addresses the challenge of deploying reinforcement learning in traffic signal control by proposing a robust multi-agent reinforcement learning framework designed to generalize across dynamic traffic conditions. The method combines three key components: turning ratio randomization to expose agents to varied traffic patterns, an exponential phase duration adjustment action space for balanced responsiveness and precision, and a neighbor-based observation scheme using MAPPO with centralized training and decentralized execution. Experiments in the Vissim simulator show the framework reduces average waiting time by over 10% compared to standard RL baselines, while demonstrating superior generalization and control stability in unseen traffic scenarios.

Human-Centred LLM Privacy Audits: Findings and Frictions
Authors: Dimitri Staufer, Kirsten Morehouse, David Hartmann, Bettina Berendt
First: 2026-03-12T16:01:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:01:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) learn statistical associations from massive training corpora and user interactions, and deployed systems can surface or infer information about individuals. Yet people lack practical ways to inspect what a model associates with their name. We report interim findings from an ongoing study and introduce LMP2, a browser-based self-audit tool. In two user studies ($N_{total}{=}458$), GPT-4o predicts 11 of 50 features for everyday people with $\ge$60\% accuracy, and participants report wanting control over LLM-generated associations despite not considering all outputs privacy violations. To validate our probing method, we evaluate eight LLMs on public figures and non-existent names, observing clear separation between stable name-conditioned associations and model defaults. Our findings also contribute to exposing a broader generative AI evaluation crisis: when outputs are probabilistic, context-dependent, and user-mediated through elicitation, what model--individual associations even include is under-specified and operationalisation relies on crafting probes and metrics that are hard to validate or compare. To move towards reliable, actionable human-centred LLM privacy audits, we identify nine frictions that emerged in our study and offer recommendations for future work and the design of human-centred LLM privacy audits.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of practical tools for users to inspect how large language models (LLMs) associate personal names with sensitive features, researchers introduced LMP2, a browser-based self-audit tool. In two user studies with 458 participants, GPT-4o accurately predicted 11 out of 50 personal features for everyday individuals with at least 60% accuracy, revealing that users desire control over LLM-generated associations even when they don’t perceive all outputs as privacy violations. Validation across eight LLMs using public figures and fabricated names showed clear distinctions between stable name-conditioned associations and default model outputs, highlighting the need for better probe design and metrics. The study also identifies nine key frictions in human-centred LLM privacy audits and calls for more rigorous, context-aware evaluation frameworks to address the ambiguity of probabilistic, user-mediated model outputs.
鉴于用户缺乏有效工具来检查大型语言模型（LLM）如何将个人姓名与敏感特征关联，本研究提出了LMP2这一基于浏览器的自我审计工具。在两项涉及458名参与者的用户研究中，GPT-4o能以至少60%的准确率预测日常个体的11项特征中的10项，而用户虽未将所有输出视为隐私侵犯，却表达了对LLM生成关联的控制需求。研究通过测试八种LLM在公众人物与虚构姓名上的表现，验证了其探针方法，发现稳定、基于姓名的关联与模型默认输出存在明显区分。研究还揭示了生成式AI评估的普遍困境：由于输出具有概率性、上下文依赖性且受用户引导，模型个体关联难以明确定义与衡量，需设计难以验证的探针与评估指标。为此，作者识别出九项审计过程中的关键摩擦点，并提出未来研究与人本化LLM隐私审计设计的建议。
Resource-Efficient Iterative LLM-Based NAS with Feedback Memory
Authors: Xiaojie Gu, Dmitry Ignatov, Radu Timofte
First: 2026-03-12T16:00:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-12T16:00:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural Architecture Search (NAS) automates network design, but conventional methods demand substantial computational resources. We propose a closed-loop pipeline leveraging large language models (LLMs) to iteratively generate, evaluate, and refine convolutional neural network architectures for image classification on a single consumer-grade GPU without LLM fine-tuning. Central to our approach is a historical feedback memory inspired by Markov chains: a sliding window of $K{=}5$ recent improvement attempts keeps context size constant while providing sufficient signal for iterative learning. Unlike prior LLM optimizers that discard failure trajectories, each history entry is a structured diagnostic triple -- recording the identified problem, suggested modification, and resulting outcome -- treating code execution failures as first-class learning signals. A dual-LLM specialization reduces per-call cognitive load: a Code Generator produces executable PyTorch architectures while a Prompt Improver handles diagnostic reasoning. Since both the LLM and architecture training share limited VRAM, the search implicitly favors compact, hardware-efficient models suited to edge deployment. We evaluate three frozen instruction-tuned LLMs (${\leq}7$B parameters) across up to 2000 iterations in an unconstrained open code space, using one-epoch proxy accuracy on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNette as a fast ranking signal. On CIFAR-10, DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B improves from 28.2% to 69.2%, Qwen2.5-7B from 50.0% to 71.5%, and GLM-5 from 43.2% to 62.0%. A full 2000-iteration search completes in ${\approx}18$ GPU hours on a single RTX~4090, establishing a low-budget, reproducible, and hardware-aware paradigm for LLM-driven NAS without cloud infrastructure.
Summary / 总结
Neural Architecture Search (NAS) automates network design, but conventional methods demand substantial computational resources.
为降低传统神经架构搜索（NAS）的高计算开销，本文提出一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的迭代式、资源高效NAS方法，可在单张消费级GPU上运行且无需微调LLM。该方法采用受马尔可夫链启发的反馈记忆机制，通过滑动窗口保留最近五次架构尝试的结构化诊断三元组（问题、修改建议、结果），以指导迭代优化。两个专用LLM分别负责生成可执行的PyTorch架构和诊断推理，降低每次调用的认知负担。在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和ImageNette上使用单轮代理准确率评估，性能显著提升：DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B从28.2%提升至69.2%，Qwen2.5-7B从50.0%提升至71.5%，GLM-5从43.2%提升至62.0%。完整2000次迭代搜索仅需约18个GPU小时（RTX 4090），无需云基础设施，实现可复现、硬件感知的LLM驱动NAS范式。
